===== backend/src/error.rs
use axum::{
    http::StatusCode,
    response::{IntoResponse, Response},
    Json,
};
use serde_json::json;

#[derive(Debug)]
pub enum AppError {
    Database(String),
    NotFound,
    Unauthorized,
}

impl IntoResponse for AppError {
    fn into_response(self) -> Response {
        let (status, error_message) = match self {
            AppError::Database(msg) => (StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR, msg),
            AppError::NotFound => (StatusCode::NOT_FOUND, "Not found".to_string()),
            AppError::Unauthorized => (StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED, "Unauthorized".to_string()),
        };

        (status, Json(json!({ "error": error_message }))).into_response()
    }
}

===== backend/src/main.rs
use axum::Router;
use std::net::SocketAddr;
use tokio::net::TcpListener;

mod auth;
mod config;
mod db;
mod error;
mod middleware;
mod routes;
mod state;

use config::Config;
use routes::router;

#[tokio::main]
async fn main() -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    // Load config
    let config = Config::from_env();
    tracing_subscriber::fmt::init();

    // Connect to database
    let db_pool = db::connect(&config.database_url).await?;

    // Build router
    let app: Router = router();

    // Bind and serve
    let addr = SocketAddr::from(([0, 0, 0, 0], config.port));
    let listener = TcpListener::bind(&addr).await?;
    
    tracing::info!("listening on {}", addr);
    axum::serve(listener, app).await?;
    Ok(())
}

===== backend/src/analytics/manager.rs
use crate::error::AppError;
use uuid::Uuid;
use sqlx::PgPool;

use super::models::*;

pub struct AnalyticsManager;

impl AnalyticsManager {
    /// Get metric data per D-074 (20 core metrics, computed from tables, no third-party)
    pub async fn get_metric(
        db: &PgPool,
        store_id: Uuid,
        metric_name: &str,
        days: i32,
    ) -> Result<MetricResponse, AppError> {
        // Compute from existing tables
        let values = match metric_name {
            "views" => {
                // Would count storefront page views (requires logging)
                vec![]
            }
            "orders" => {
                let rows: Vec<(String, i64)> = sqlx::query_as(
                    r#"
                    SELECT DATE(created_at)::text as date, COUNT(*) as count
                    FROM app.order
                    WHERE store_id = $1 AND created_at > now() - interval '$2 days'
                    GROUP BY DATE(created_at)
                    ORDER BY date
                    "#,
                )
                .bind(&store_id)
                .bind(days)
                .fetch_all(db)
                .await
                .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

                rows.into_iter()
                    .map(|(date, count)| MetricValue {
                        date,
                        value: count,
                    })
                    .collect()
            }
            "revenue" => {
                let rows: Vec<(String, i64)> = sqlx::query_as(
                    r#"
                    SELECT DATE(created_at)::text as date, SUM((order_snapshot ->> 'total_egp')::bigint) as revenue
                    FROM app.order
                    WHERE store_id = $1 AND created_at > now() - interval '$2 days'
                    GROUP BY DATE(created_at)
                    ORDER BY date
                    "#,
                )
                .bind(&store_id)
                .bind(days)
                .fetch_all(db)
                .await
                .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

                rows.into_iter()
                    .map(|(date, revenue)| MetricValue {
                        date,
                        value: revenue.unwrap_or(0),
                    })
                    .collect()
            }
            _ => vec![],
        };

        let total: i64 = values.iter().map(|v| v.value).sum();

        Ok(MetricResponse {
            metric_name: metric_name.to_string(),
            values,
            current_period_total: total,
        })
    }

    /// Get dashboard summary per D-074
    pub async fn get_dashboard_summary(
        db: &PgPool,
        store_id: Uuid,
        days: i32,
    ) -> Result<DashboardMetricsResponse, AppError> {
        // Total orders
        let (total_orders,): (i64,) = sqlx::query_as(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM app.order WHERE store_id = $1 AND created_at > now() - interval '$2 days'",
        )
        .bind(&store_id)
        .bind(days)
        .fetch_one(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        // Total revenue
        let (total_revenue,): (Option<i64>,) = sqlx::query_as(
            r#"
            SELECT SUM((order_snapshot ->> 'total_egp')::bigint)
            FROM app.order
            WHERE store_id = $1 AND created_at > now() - interval '$2 days'
            "#,
        )
        .bind(&store_id)
        .bind(days)
        .fetch_one(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        let total_revenue_egp = total_revenue.unwrap_or(0);
        let avg_order_value = if total_orders > 0 {
            total_revenue_egp / total_orders
        } else {
            0
        };

        // Top products
        let top_products: Vec<TopProduct> = sqlx::query_as(
            r#"
            SELECT 
                p.id::text as product_id,
                p.name as product_name,
                SUM((oi.quantity)::bigint) as units_sold,
                SUM((oi.quantity * oi.unit_price_egp)::bigint) as revenue_egp
            FROM app.order_item oi
            JOIN app.product p ON oi.product_id = p.id
            JOIN app.order o ON oi.order_id = o.id
            WHERE o.store_id = $1 AND o.created_at > now() - interval '$2 days'
            GROUP BY p.id, p.name
            ORDER BY revenue_egp DESC
            LIMIT 5
            "#,
        )
        .bind(&store_id)
        .bind(days)
        .fetch_all(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        Ok(DashboardMetricsResponse {
            total_views: 0, // Would require view logging
            total_orders,
            total_revenue_egp,
            average_order_value_egp: avg_order_value,
            conversion_rate: 0.0, // Would require view logging
            top_products,
            period: format!("last {} days", days),
        })
    }
}

===== backend/src/analytics/routes.rs
use axum::{
    extract::{Path, Query, State},
    routing::get,
    Json, Router,
};
use serde::Deserialize;
use uuid::Uuid;

use crate::auth::SessionClaim;
use crate::error::AppError;
use crate::state::AppState;

use super::manager::AnalyticsManager;
use super::models::*;

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
pub struct MetricQuery {
    pub metric: String,
    pub days: Option<i32>,
}

pub fn analytics_routes() -> Router<AppState> {
    Router::new()
        .route("/stores/:store_id/analytics/dashboard", get(get_dashboard))
        .route("/stores/:store_id/analytics/metric", get(get_metric))
}

async fn get_dashboard(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim { account_id, .. }: SessionClaim,
    Path(store_id): Path<Uuid>,
    Query(params): Query<MetricQuery>,
) -> Result<Json<DashboardMetricsResponse>, AppError> {
    // TODO: Verify store ownership
    let summary = AnalyticsManager::get_dashboard_summary(
        &state.db,
        store_id,
        params.days.unwrap_or(30),
    )
    .await?;

    Ok(Json(summary))
}

async fn get_metric(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim { account_id, .. }: SessionClaim,
    Path(store_id): Path<Uuid>,
    Query(params): Query<MetricQuery>,
) -> Result<Json<MetricResponse>, AppError> {
    // TODO: Verify store ownership
    let metric = AnalyticsManager::get_metric(
        &state.db,
        store_id,
        &params.metric,
        params.days.unwrap_or(30),
    )
    .await?;

    Ok(Json(metric))
}

===== backend/src/auth/attempt_token.rs
// backend/src/auth/attempt_token.rs

//! Login attempt state machine in Valkey per D-015, D-023.

use crate::error::{AppError, AppResult};
use redis::aio::ConnectionManager;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use uuid::Uuid;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum AttemptState {
    Issued,
    PasswordOk,
    EnrollmentPending,
    MfaOk,
    SessionIssued,
    Expired,
    Burned,
    Failed,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct AttemptToken {
    pub token: String,
    pub state: AttemptState,
    pub account_id: Option<Uuid>,
    pub created_at: i64,
    pub ip_address: Option<String>,
}

impl AttemptToken {
    /// Create new attempt token (ISSUED state) per D-015.
    pub async fn issue(
        cache: &ConnectionManager,
        ttl_secs: u64,
        ip_address: Option<String>,
    ) -> AppResult<String> {
        let token = uuid::Uuid::new_v4().to_string();
        let key = format!("attempt_token:{}", token);
        
        let attempt = AttemptToken {
            token: token.clone(),
            state: AttemptState::Issued,
            account_id: None,
            created_at: chrono::Utc::now().timestamp(),
            ip_address,
        };
        
        let mut conn = cache.clone();
        redis::cmd("SET")
            .arg(&key)
            .arg(serde_json::to_string(&attempt)?)
            .arg("EX")
            .arg(ttl_secs)
            .query_async::<_, ()>(&mut conn)
            .await?;
        
        Ok(token)
    }

    /// Transition to PASSWORD_OK state and mint new token per D-015.
    pub async fn transition_to_password_ok(
        cache: &ConnectionManager,
        token: &str,
        account_id: Uuid,
        ttl_secs: u64,
    ) -> AppResult<String> {
        let key = format!("attempt_token:{}", token);
        let mut attempt: AttemptToken = Self::get(cache, token).await?;
        
        // Burn old token
        let mut conn = cache.clone();
        redis::cmd("DEL")
            .arg(&key)
            .query_async::<_, ()>(&mut conn)
            .await?;
        
        // Mint new token
        let new_token = uuid::Uuid::new_v4().to_string();
        let new_key = format!("attempt_token:{}", new_token);
        
        attempt.token = new_token.clone();
        attempt.state = AttemptState::PasswordOk;
        attempt.account_id = Some(account_id);
        attempt.created_at = chrono::Utc::now().timestamp();
        
        redis::cmd("SET")
            .arg(&new_key)
            .arg(serde_json::to_string(&attempt)?)
            .arg("EX")
            .arg(ttl_secs)
            .query_async::<_, ()>(&mut conn)
            .await?;
        
        Ok(new_token)
    }

    /// Get attempt state from Valkey.
    pub async fn get(cache: &ConnectionManager, token: &str) -> AppResult<Self> {
        let key = format!("attempt_token:{}", token);
        let mut conn = cache.clone();
        
        let data: Option<String> = redis::cmd("GET")
            .arg(&key)
            .query_async(&mut conn)
            .await?;
        
        match data {
            Some(json) => serde_json::from_str(&json)
                .map_err(|_| AppError::InvalidToken),
            None => Err(AppError::InvalidToken),
        }
    }

    /// Burn token (terminal state produces non-authorizing audit record).
    pub async fn burn(cache: &ConnectionManager, token: &str) -> AppResult<()> {
        let key = format!("attempt_token:{}", token);
        let mut conn = cache.clone();
        redis::cmd("DEL")
            .arg(&key)
            .query_async::<_, ()>(&mut conn)
            .await?;
        Ok(())
    }
}

===== backend/src/auth/errors.rs
// backend/src/auth/errors.rs

//! Authentication-specific error handling with uniform timing per D-012.

use crate::error::AppError;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

/// Uniform timing for authentication failures per D-012.
pub async fn uniform_auth_delay() {
    // Constant delay to prevent timing attacks
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(100)).await;
}

/// Log authentication failure with protocol violation detection per D-012.
pub fn log_auth_failure(
    event_type: &str,
    ip_address: Option<&str>,
    account_id: Option<&str>,
    is_protocol_violation: bool,
) {
    let event_level = if is_protocol_violation {
        "protocol_violation"
    } else {
        "auth_failure"
    };
    
    tracing::warn!(
        event = event_level,
        event_type = event_type,
        ip_address = ip_address,
        account_id = account_id,
        "Authentication event"
    );
}

/// Return generic auth failure response.
pub fn generic_auth_error() -> AppError {
    AppError::AuthenticationFailed
}

===== backend/src/authorization/capability.rs
// backend/src/authorization/capability.rs

//! Permission catalog loader and capability checking per D-009.

use crate::error::{AppError, AppResult};
use sqlx::PgPool;
use std::collections::HashMap;
use uuid::Uuid;

/// Complete permission catalog per D-009.
pub struct CapabilityChecker {
    capabilities: HashMap<String, CapabilityDef>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct CapabilityDef {
    pub name: String,
    pub description: String,
    pub is_delegable: bool,
    pub is_dual_control: bool,
}

impl CapabilityChecker {
    /// Load capability catalog from database.
    pub async fn load(db: &PgPool) -> AppResult<Self> {
        let rows = sqlx::query_as::<_, (String, String, bool, bool)>(
            r#"
            SELECT capability_name, description, is_delegable, is_dual_control
            FROM app.permission_catalog
            ORDER BY capability_name
            "#,
        )
        .fetch_all(db)
        .await?;

        let mut capabilities = HashMap::new();
        for (name, description, is_delegable, is_dual_control) in rows {
            capabilities.insert(
                name.clone(),
                CapabilityDef {
                    name,
                    description,
                    is_delegable,
                    is_dual_control,
                },
            );
        }

        Ok(Self { capabilities })
    }

    /// Check if capability exists in catalog.
    pub fn has_capability(&self, capability: &str) -> bool {
        self.capabilities.contains_key(capability)
    }

    /// Get capability definition.
    pub fn get(&self, capability: &str) -> Option<&CapabilityDef> {
        self.capabilities.get(capability)
    }

    /// List all capabilities.
    pub fn list_all(&self) -> Vec<&CapabilityDef> {
        self.capabilities.values().collect()
    }

    /// Check if capability is delegable per D-009.
    pub fn is_delegable(&self, capability: &str) -> bool {
        self.capabilities
            .get(capability)
            .map(|c| c.is_delegable)
            .unwrap_or(false)
    }

    /// Check if capability requires dual-control per D-026.
    pub fn requires_dual_control(&self, capability: &str) -> bool {
        self.capabilities
            .get(capability)
            .map(|c| c.is_dual_control)
            .unwrap_or(false)
    }
}

pub fn default_capabilities() -> Vec<(&'static str, &'static str, bool, bool)> {

===== backend/src/authorization/check.rs
// backend/src/authorization/check.rs

//! Authorization check: (account_id, current_context, capability) per D-004.

use crate::error::{AppError, AppResult};
use sqlx::PgPool;
use uuid::Uuid;

/// Per-request authorization context.
pub struct AuthorizationCheck {
    pub account_id: Uuid,
    pub context_id: Uuid,
    pub capability: String,
}

impl AuthorizationCheck {
    /// Check authorization in database: does account have capability in context?
    /// Per D-004, this checks (account_id, context_id) + capability, not account_id alone.
    pub async fn execute(&self, db: &PgPool) -> AppResult<()> {
        let has_capability = sqlx::query_scalar::<_, bool>(
            r#"
            SELECT EXISTS(
                SELECT 1 FROM app.account_permission ap
                JOIN app.permission_catalog pc ON ap.capability_id = pc.id
                WHERE ap.account_id = $1
                AND ap.context_id = $2
                AND pc.capability_name = $3
                AND (ap.expires_at IS NULL OR ap.expires_at > now())
                AND ap.created_at <= now()
            )
            "#,
        )
        .bind(self.account_id)
        .bind(self.context_id)
        .bind(&self.capability)
        .fetch_one(db)
        .await?;

        if has_capability {
            Ok(())
        } else {
            Err(AppError::Forbidden(format!(
                "Missing capability: {}",
                self.capability
            )))
        }
    }

    /// Check without context_id (for testing privilege bleed vulnerability).
    /// This is DELIBERATELY BROKEN and used in tests to prove the vulnerability.
    #[allow(dead_code)]
    async fn execute_broken_no_context(&self, db: &PgPool) -> AppResult<()> {
        // BUG: This only checks account_id and capability, ignoring context_id.
        // This is intentionally broken for testing privilege bleed.
        let has_capability = sqlx::query_scalar::<_, bool>(
            r#"
            SELECT EXISTS(
                SELECT 1 FROM app.account_permission ap
                JOIN app.permission_catalog pc ON ap.capability_id = pc.id
                WHERE ap.account_id = $1
                AND pc.capability_name = $2
                AND (ap.expires_at IS NULL OR ap.expires_at > now())
            )
            "#,
        )
        .bind(self.account_id)
        .bind(&self.capability)
        .fetch_one(db)
        .await?;

        if has_capability {
            Ok(())
        } else {
            Err(AppError::Forbidden(format!(
                "Missing capability: {}",
                self.capability
            )))
        }
    }
}

===== backend/src/authorization/context.rs
// backend/src/authorization/context.rs

//! Context switching and claim reissue per D-004, D-023.

use crate::error::{AppError, AppResult};
use crate::auth::session::SessionClaim;
use sqlx::PgPool;
use uuid::Uuid;

pub struct ContextManager;

impl ContextManager {
    /// Switch context and reissue session claim per D-004.
    /// Atomically reads live bindings and permission version.
    pub async fn switch_context(
        db: &PgPool,
        account_id: Uuid,
        new_context_id: Uuid,
    ) -> AppResult<SessionClaim> {
        // Verify the new context binding exists
        let (role, permission_version): (String, i64) = sqlx::query_as(
            r#"
            SELECT ar.role_name, COALESCE(MAX(ap.version), 1)
            FROM app.account_role ar
            LEFT JOIN app.account_permission ap ON ap.account_id = ar.account_id
            AND ar.context_id = ap.context_id
            WHERE ar.account_id = $1
            AND ar.context_id = $2
            AND ar.deleted_at IS NULL
            GROUP BY ar.role_name
            "#,
        )
        .bind(account_id)
        .bind(new_context_id)
        .fetch_optional(db)
        .await?
        .ok_or_else(|| {
            AppError::Forbidden("Context binding not found or revoked".to_string())
        })?;

        // Create new claim with updated context
        let now = chrono::Utc::now();
        let expires = now + chrono::Duration::days(30);

        Ok(SessionClaim {
            session_id: Uuid::new_v4(),
            account_id,
            context_id: new_context_id,
            role,
            permission_version,
            issued_at: now,
            expires_at: expires,
        })
    }

    /// Detect permission revocation via version mismatch per D-004.
    pub async fn check_permission_version(
        db: &PgPool,
        account_id: Uuid,
        context_id: Uuid,
        claimed_version: i64,
    ) -> AppResult<()> {
        let actual_version: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar(
            r#"
            SELECT COALESCE(MAX(version), 1)
            FROM app.account_permission
            WHERE account_id = $1 AND context_id = $2
            "#,
        )
        .bind(account_id)
        .bind(context_id)
        .fetch_one(db)
        .await?;

        if claimed_version < actual_version {
            // Permissions were revoked
            return Err(AppError::Unauthorized);
        }

        Ok(())
    }
}

===== backend/src/auth/password.rs
// backend/src/auth/password.rs

//! Password hashing with Argon2id and HIBP k-anonymity check per D-047.

use crate::error::{AppError, AppResult};
use argon2::{Argon2, PasswordHash, PasswordHasher, PasswordVerifier};
use argon2::password_hash::SaltString;
use sha1::{Sha1, Digest};
use anyhow::anyhow;

/// Password manager with HIBP integration.
pub struct PasswordManager;

impl PasswordManager {
    /// Hash password using Argon2id per D-047.
    pub fn hash_password(password: &str) -> AppResult<String> {
        let salt = SaltString::generate(rand::thread_rng());
        let argon2 = Argon2::default();
        
        argon2
            .hash_password(password.as_bytes(), &salt)
            .map(|hash| hash.to_string())
            .map_err(|e| AppError::InternalError(anyhow!("Password hashing failed: {}", e)))
    }

    /// Verify password against hash.
    pub fn verify_password(password: &str, hash: &str) -> AppResult<bool> {
        let parsed_hash = PasswordHash::new(hash)
            .map_err(|e| AppError::InternalError(anyhow!("Invalid password hash: {}", e)))?;
        
        let argon2 = Argon2::default();
        match argon2.verify_password(password.as_bytes(), &parsed_hash) {
            Ok(()) => Ok(true),
            Err(_) => Ok(false),
        }
    }

    /// Check password against HaveIBeenPwned via k-anonymity per D-047.
    pub async fn check_hibp_breach(password: &str) -> AppResult<bool> {
        // Compute SHA-1 hash
        let mut hasher = Sha1::new();
        hasher.update(password.as_bytes());
        let digest = hasher.finalize();
        let sha1_hex = format!("{:x}", digest).to_uppercase();
        
        // Send first 5 chars only (k-anonymity)
        let prefix = &sha1_hex[..5];
        let suffix = &sha1_hex[5..];
        
        // Call HaveIBeenPwned API
        let client = reqwest::Client::new();
        match client
            .get(&format!("https://api.pwnedpasswords.com/range/{}", prefix))
            .header("User-Agent", "Rabita-Backend")
            .send()
            .await
        {
            Ok(resp) => {
                let text = resp.text().await.unwrap_or_default();
                Ok(text.contains(suffix))
            }
            Err(_) => {
                // Network error: fail open for user experience
                tracing::warn!("HIBP check failed, allowing password");
                Ok(false)
            }
        }
    }

    /// Enforce password policy per D-111: 12+ chars, no complexity rules.
    pub fn validate_password_policy(password: &str) -> AppResult<()> {
        if password.len() < 12 {
            return Err(AppError::BadRequest(
                "Password must be at least 12 characters".to_string(),
            ));
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

===== backend/src/auth/session.rs
// backend/src/auth/session.rs

//! Session management with rotation and revocation per D-014.

use crate::{error::{AppError, AppResult}, state::AppState};
use redis::aio::ConnectionManager;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use uuid::Uuid;
use chrono::{DateTime, Utc, Duration};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct SessionClaim {
    pub session_id: Uuid,
    pub account_id: Uuid,
    pub context_id: Uuid,
    pub role: String,
    pub permission_version: i64,
    pub issued_at: DateTime<Utc>,
    pub expires_at: DateTime<Utc>,
}

pub struct SessionManager;

impl SessionManager {
    /// Issue new session.
    pub async fn issue_session(
        cache: &ConnectionManager,
        account_id: Uuid,
        context_id: Uuid,
        role: String,
        absolute_ttl_secs: u64,
    ) -> AppResult<(String, SessionClaim)> {
        let session_id = Uuid::new_v4();
        let token = uuid::Uuid::new_v4().to_string();
        let now = Utc::now();
        let expires = now + Duration::seconds(absolute_ttl_secs as i64);
        
        let claim = SessionClaim {
            session_id,
            account_id,
            context_id,
            role,
            permission_version: 1,
            issued_at: now,
            expires_at: expires,
        };
        
        let key = format!("session:{}", token);
        let mut conn = cache.clone();
        
        redis::cmd("SET")
            .arg(&key)
            .arg(serde_json::to_string(&claim)?)
            .arg("EX")
            .arg(absolute_ttl_secs)
            .query_async::<_, ()>(&mut conn)
            .await?;
        
        Ok((token, claim))
    }

    /// Verify and retrieve session.
    pub async fn verify_session(cache: &ConnectionManager, token: &str) -> AppResult<SessionClaim> {
        let key = format!("session:{}", token);
        let mut conn = cache.clone();
        
        let data: Option<String> = redis::cmd("GET")
            .arg(&key)
            .query_async(&mut conn)
            .await?;
        
        match data {
            Some(json) => {
                let claim: SessionClaim = serde_json::from_str(&json)?;
                
                // Check expiry
                if Utc::now() > claim.expires_at {
                    redis::cmd("DEL")
                        .arg(&key)
                        .query_async::<_, ()>(&mut conn)
                        .await?;
                    return Err(AppError::SessionExpired);
                }
                
                Ok(claim)
            }
            None => Err(AppError::SessionExpired),
        }
    }

    /// Rotate session token.
    pub async fn rotate_session(
        cache: &ConnectionManager,
        old_token: &str,
        absolute_ttl_secs: u64,
    ) -> AppResult<String> {
        let claim = Self::verify_session(cache, old_token).await?;
        let new_token = uuid::Uuid::new_v4().to_string();
        
        let old_key = format!("session:{}", old_token);
        let new_key = format!("session:{}", new_token);
        
        let mut conn = cache.clone();
        
        // Delete old
        redis::cmd("DEL")
            .arg(&old_key)
            .query_async::<_, ()>(&mut conn)
            .await?;
        
        // Create new with same claim
        redis::cmd("SET")
            .arg(&new_key)
            .arg(serde_json::to_string(&claim)?)
            .arg("EX")
            .arg(absolute_ttl_secs)
            .query_async::<_, ()>(&mut conn)
            .await?;
        
        Ok(new_token)
    }

    /// Revoke session.
    pub async fn revoke_session(cache: &ConnectionManager, token: &str) -> AppResult<()> {
        let key = format!("session:{}", token);
        let mut conn = cache.clone();
        
        redis::cmd("DEL")
            .arg(&key)
            .query_async::<_, ()>(&mut conn)
            .await?;
        
        Ok(())
    }
}

===== backend/src/auth/totp.rs
// backend/src/auth/totp.rs

//! TOTP enrollment and verification with replay prevention per D-047.

use crate::error::{AppError, AppResult};
use totp_rs::{TOTP, Secret, Algorithm};

pub struct TOTPManager;

impl TOTPManager {
    /// Generate new TOTP secret for enrollment.
    pub fn generate_secret(account_id: &str) -> AppResult<(String, String)> {
        let secret = Secret::generate_secret();
        let totp = TOTP::new(
            Algorithm::SHA1,
            6,
            1,
            30,
            secret.to_bytes()?,
            Some("Rabita".to_string()),
            account_id.to_string(),
        )?;
        
        let url = totp.get_url();
        Ok((secret.to_string(), url))
    }

    /// Verify TOTP code with replay prevention per D-047.
    pub fn verify_code(
        secret: &str,
        code: &str,
        last_accepted_step: Option<i64>,
    ) -> AppResult<(bool, i64)> {
        let totp = TOTP::new(
            Algorithm::SHA1,
            6,
            1,
            30,
            Secret::Encoded(secret.to_string()).to_bytes()?,
            Some("Rabita".to_string()),
            "user".to_string(),
        )?;
        
        match totp.check_current(code) {
            Ok(()) => {
                let current_step = (std::time::SystemTime::now()
                    .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)?
                    .as_secs() / 30) as i64;
                
                // Reject if same step as last accepted
                if let Some(last_step) = last_accepted_step {
                    if current_step == last_step {
                        return Err(AppError::BadRequest("Code already used".to_string()));
                    }
                }
                
                Ok((true, current_step))
            }
            Err(_) => Ok((false, 0)),
        }
    }
}

===== backend/src/auth/webauthn.rs
// backend/src/auth/webauthn.rs

//! WebAuthn/FIDO2 registration and assertion per D-013.

use crate::error::{AppError, AppResult};
use webauthn_rs::prelude::*;
use uuid::Uuid;

pub struct WebAuthnManager {
    rp_id: String,
    rp_origin: Url,
}

impl WebAuthnManager {
    pub fn new(rp_id: String, rp_origin_str: String) -> AppResult<Self> {
        let rp_origin = Url::parse(&rp_origin_str)
            .map_err(|_| AppError::BadRequest("Invalid origin URL".to_string()))?;
        
        Ok(Self {
            rp_id,
            rp_origin,
        })
    }

    /// Start registration ceremony.
    pub fn registration_challenge(
        &self,
        user_id: &Uuid,
        user_name: &str,
    ) -> AppResult<(CreationChallengeResponse, Vec<u8>)> {
        let rp = Webauthn::new(
            &self.rp_id,
            &self.rp_origin,
            &[self.rp_origin.clone()],
        )
        .map_err(|e| AppError::InternalError(anyhow::anyhow!("WebAuthn init: {}", e)))?;
        
        let user_id_bytes = user_id.as_bytes().to_vec();
        let (challenge, registration_state) = rp
            .start_passkey_registration(
                user_id_bytes.clone().into(),
                user_name,
                user_name,
                None,
            )
            .map_err(|e| AppError::InternalError(anyhow::anyhow!("WebAuthn challenge: {}", e)))?;
        
        let state_bytes = serde_json::to_vec(&registration_state)?;
        
        Ok((challenge.public_key, state_bytes))
    }

    /// Verify registration response.
    pub fn finish_registration(
        &self,
        challenge_state: &[u8],
        response: &RegisterPublicKeyCredential,
    ) -> AppResult<Vec<u8>> {
        let rp = Webauthn::new(
            &self.rp_id,
            &self.rp_origin,
            &[self.rp_origin.clone()],
        )?;
        
        let reg_state: PasskeyRegistrationState = serde_json::from_slice(challenge_state)?;
        let credential = rp.finish_passkey_registration(response, &reg_state)?;
        
        Ok(credential.cred_public_key.to_vec())
    }
}

===== backend/src/branches/manager.rs
use crate::db::Queries;
use crate::error::{AppError, ErrorCode};
use crate::audit::AuditLogger;
use uuid::Uuid;
use sqlx::PgPool;
use chrono::Utc;

use super::models::*;

pub struct BranchManager;

impl BranchManager {
    /// Create a branch for a store.
    pub async fn create_branch(
        pool: &PgPool,
        account_id: Uuid,
        store_id: Uuid,
        req: CreateBranchRequest,
    ) -> Result<Branch, AppError> {
        // Verify store ownership
        let _store: (Uuid,) = sqlx::query_as(
            "SELECT id FROM app.store WHERE id = $1 AND account_id = $2 AND deleted_at IS NULL",
        )
        .bind(&store_id)
        .bind(&account_id)
        .fetch_optional(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?
        .ok_or_else(|| AppError::new(ErrorCode::NotFound, "Store not found"))?;

        let branch_id = Uuid::new_v4();
        let now = Utc::now();

        sqlx::query(
            r#"
            INSERT INTO app.branch (id, store_id, name, address, governorate, county, district, phone, is_published, allow_pickup, created_at, updated_at)
            VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, $7, $8, false, $9, $10, $10)
            "#,
        )
        .bind(&branch_id)
        .bind(&store_id)
        .bind(&req.name)
        .bind(&req.address)
        .bind(&req.governorate)
        .bind(&req.county)
        .bind(&req.district)
        .bind(&req.phone)
        .bind(req.allow_pickup.unwrap_or(false))
        .bind(&now)
        .execute(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        // Initialize working hours (all closed by default)
        for day in 0..7 {
            sqlx::query(
                "INSERT INTO app.branch_working_hours (id, branch_id, day_of_week, is_closed, open_time, close_time) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, true, NULL, NULL)"
            )
            .bind(Uuid::new_v4())
            .bind(&branch_id)
            .bind(day)
            .execute(pool)
            .await
            .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;
        }

        AuditLogger::log_action(
            pool,
            account_id,
            "branch.create",
            Some(branch_id),
            serde_json::json!(&req),
            None,
        )
        .await?;

        Self::get_branch(pool, account_id, store_id, branch_id).await
    }

    /// Get branch (validate store ownership).
    pub async fn get_branch(
        pool: &PgPool,
        account_id: Uuid,
        store_id: Uuid,
        branch_id: Uuid,
    ) -> Result<Branch, AppError> {
        // Verify store ownership first
        let _: (Uuid,) = sqlx::query_as(
            "SELECT id FROM app.store WHERE id = $1 AND account_id = $2 AND deleted_at IS NULL",
        )
        .bind(&store_id)
        .bind(&account_id)
        .fetch_optional(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?
        .ok_or_else(|| AppError::new(ErrorCode::NotFound, "Store not found"))?;

        sqlx::query_as::<_, Branch>(
            "SELECT * FROM app.branch WHERE id = $1 AND store_id = $2 AND deleted_at IS NULL",
        )
        .bind(&branch_id)
        .bind(&store_id)
        .fetch_optional(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?
        .ok_or_else(|| AppError::new(ErrorCode::NotFound, "Branch not found"))
    }

    /// List branches for store.
    pub async fn list_branches(
        pool: &PgPool,
        account_id: Uuid,
        store_id: Uuid,
    ) -> Result<Vec<Branch>, AppError> {
        // Verify store ownership
        let _: (Uuid,) = sqlx::query_as(
            "SELECT id FROM app.store WHERE id = $1 AND account_id = $2 AND deleted_at IS NULL",
        )
        .bind(&store_id)
        .bind(&account_id)
        .fetch_optional(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?
        .ok_or_else(|| AppError::new(ErrorCode::NotFound, "Store not found"))?;

        sqlx::query_as::<_, Branch>(
            "SELECT * FROM app.branch WHERE store_id = $1 AND deleted_at IS NULL ORDER BY name",
        )
        .bind(&store_id)
        .fetch_all(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))
    }

    /// Update branch.
    pub async fn update_branch(
        pool: &PgPool,
        account_id: Uuid,
        store_id: Uuid,
        branch_id: Uuid,
        req: UpdateBranchRequest,
    ) -> Result<Branch, AppError> {
        let _branch = Self::get_branch(pool, account_id, store_id, branch_id).await?;
        let now = Utc::now();

        sqlx::query(
            r#"
            UPDATE app.branch
            SET name = COALESCE($1, name),
                address = COALESCE($2, address),
                governorate = COALESCE($3, governorate),
                county = COALESCE($4, county),
                district = COALESCE($5, district),
                phone = COALESCE($6, phone),
                allow_pickup = COALESCE($7, allow_pickup),
                updated_at = $8
            WHERE id = $9
            "#,
        )
        .bind(&req.name)
        .bind(&req.address)
        .bind(&req.governorate)
        .bind(&req.county)
        .bind(&req.district)
        .bind(&req.phone)
        .bind(&req.allow_pickup)
        .bind(&now)
        .bind(&branch_id)
        .execute(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        AuditLogger::log_action(
            pool,
            account_id,
            "branch.update",
            Some(branch_id),
            serde_json::json!(&req),
            None,
        )
        .await?;

        Self::get_branch(pool, account_id, store_id, branch_id).await
    }

    /// Set working hours for branch (replace existing).
    pub async fn set_working_hours(
        pool: &PgPool,
        account_id: Uuid,
        store_id: Uuid,
        branch_id: Uuid,
        req: SetWorkingHoursRequest,
    ) -> Result<Vec<BranchWorkingHours>, AppError> {
        let _branch = Self::get_branch(pool, account_id, store_id, branch_id).await?;

        let mut tx = pool.begin().await.map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        // Delete existing hours
        sqlx::query("DELETE FROM app.branch_working_hours WHERE branch_id = $1")
            .bind(&branch_id)
            .execute(&mut *tx)
            .await
            .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        // Insert new hours
        for hour in &req.hours {
            sqlx::query(
                "INSERT INTO app.branch_working_hours (id, branch_id, day_of_week, is_closed, open_time, close_time) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6)"
            )
            .bind(Uuid::new_v4())
            .bind(&branch_id)
            .bind(hour.day_of_week)
            .bind(hour.is_closed)
            .bind(&hour.open_time)
            .bind(&hour.close_time)
            .execute(&mut *tx)
            .await
            .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;
        }

        AuditLogger::log_action(
            &mut *tx,
            account_id,
            "branch.set_working_hours",
            Some(branch_id),
            serde_json::json!(&req),
            None,
        )
        .await?;

        tx.commit().await.map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        sqlx::query_as::<_, BranchWorkingHours>(
            "SELECT * FROM app.branch_working_hours WHERE branch_id = $1 ORDER BY day_of_week",
        )
        .bind(&branch_id)
        .fetch_all(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))
    }

    /// Adjust stock (inventory.adjust permission).
    pub async fn adjust_stock(
        pool: &PgPool,
        account_id: Uuid,
        store_id: Uuid,
        branch_id: Uuid,
        req: AdjustStockRequest,
    ) -> Result<BranchProduct, AppError> {
        // Verify store and branch ownership
        let _branch = Self::get_branch(pool, account_id, store_id, branch_id).await?;

        // Verify product belongs to store
        let _: (Uuid,) = sqlx::query_as(
            "SELECT id FROM app.product WHERE id = $1 AND store_id = $2",
        )
        .bind(&req.product_id)
        .bind(&store_id)
        .fetch_optional(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?
        .ok_or_else(|| AppError::new(ErrorCode::NotFound, "Product not found"))?;

        let mut tx = pool.begin().await.map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        // Get or create branch_product record
        let bp_id: Option<(Uuid,)> = sqlx::query_as(
            "SELECT id FROM app.branch_product WHERE branch_id = $1 AND product_id = $2",
        )
        .bind(&branch_id)
        .bind(&req.product_id)
        .fetch_optional(&mut *tx)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        let bp_id = if let Some((id,)) = bp_id {
            id
        } else {
            let new_id = Uuid::new_v4();
            sqlx::query(
                "INSERT INTO app.branch_product (id, branch_id, product_id, stock_qty, is_published) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, 0, false)"
            )
            .bind(&new_id)
            .bind(&branch_id)
            .bind(&req.product_id)
            .execute(&mut *tx)
            .await
            .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;
            new_id
        };

        // Update stock
        let now = Utc::now();
        sqlx::query(
            "UPDATE app.branch_product SET stock_qty = stock_qty + $1, updated_at = $2 WHERE id = $3",
        )
        .bind(req.quantity_delta)
        .bind(&now)
        .bind(&bp_id)
        .execute(&mut *tx)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        // Audit
        AuditLogger::log_action(
            &mut *tx,
            account_id,
            "inventory.adjust",
            Some(bp_id),
            serde_json::json!({ "delta": req.quantity_delta, "reason": req.reason }),
            None,
        )
        .await?;

        tx.commit().await.map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        sqlx::query_as::<_, BranchProduct>(
            "SELECT * FROM app.branch_product WHERE id = $1",
        )
        .bind(&bp_id)
        .fetch_one(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))
    }
}

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use uuid::Uuid;
use chrono::{DateTime, Utc, NaiveTime};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, sqlx::FromRow)]
pub struct Branch {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub store_id: Uuid,
    pub name: String,
    pub address: String,
    pub governorate: String,
    pub county: String,
    pub district: String,
    pub phone: String,
    pub is_published: bool,
    pub allow_pickup: bool,
    pub created_at: DateTime<Utc>,
    pub updated_at: DateTime<Utc>,
    pub deleted_at: Option<DateTime<Utc>>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, sqlx::FromRow)]
pub struct BranchWorkingHours {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub branch_id: Uuid,
    pub day_of_week: i32, // 0 = Monday, 6 = Sunday
    pub open_time: Option<String>, // HH:MM or NULL if closed
    pub close_time: Option<String>,
    pub is_closed: bool,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, sqlx::FromRow)]
pub struct BranchProduct {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub branch_id: Uuid,
    pub product_id: Uuid,
    pub stock_qty: i64, // CHECK (stock_qty >= 0)
    pub is_published: bool,
    pub updated_at: DateTime<Utc>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
pub struct CreateBranchRequest {
    pub name: String,
    pub address: String,
    pub governorate: String,
    pub county: String,
    pub district: String,
    pub phone: String,
    pub allow_pickup: Option<bool>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
pub struct UpdateBranchRequest {
    pub name: Option<String>,
    pub address: Option<String>,
    pub governorate: Option<String>,
    pub county: Option<String>,
    pub district: Option<String>,
    pub phone: Option<String>,
    pub allow_pickup: Option<bool>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
pub struct SetWorkingHoursRequest {
    pub hours: Vec<WorkingHourInput>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
pub struct WorkingHourInput {
    pub day_of_week: i32,
    pub open_time: Option<String>, // "09:00"
    pub close_time: Option<String>,
    pub is_closed: bool,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
pub struct AdjustStockRequest {
    pub product_id: Uuid,
    pub quantity_delta: i64,
    pub reason: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
pub struct PublishBranchProductRequest {
    pub product_ids: Vec<Uuid>,
    pub publish: bool,
}

#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct BranchResponse {
    pub id: String,
    pub store_id: String,
    pub name: String,
    pub address: String,
    pub governorate: String,
    pub county: String,
    pub district: String,
    pub phone: String,
    pub is_published: bool,
    pub allow_pickup: bool,
    pub working_hours: Vec<BranchWorkingHours>,
    pub created_at: String,
}

use axum::{
    extract::{Path, State},
    http::StatusCode,
    routing::{get, post, put},
    Json, Router,
};
use uuid::Uuid;

use crate::auth::SessionClaim;
use crate::error::AppError;
use crate::state::AppState;

use super::manager::BranchManager;
use super::models::*;

pub fn branch_routes() -> Router<AppState> {
    Router::new()
        .route(
            "/stores/:store_id/branches",
            post(create_branch).get(list_branches),
        )
        .route(
            "/stores/:store_id/branches/:branch_id",
            get(get_branch).put(update_branch),
        )
        .route(
            "/stores/:store_id/branches/:branch_id/working-hours",
            put(set_working_hours),
        )
        .route(
            "/stores/:store_id/branches/:branch_id/stock/adjust",
            post(adjust_stock),
        )
}

async fn create_branch(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim { account_id, .. }: SessionClaim,
    Path(store_id): Path<Uuid>,
    Json(req): Json<CreateBranchRequest>,
) -> Result<(StatusCode, Json<Branch>), AppError> {
    let branch = BranchManager::create_branch(&state.db, account_id, store_id, req).await?;
    Ok((StatusCode::CREATED, Json(branch)))
}

async fn list_branches(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim { account_id, .. }: SessionClaim,
    Path(store_id): Path<Uuid>,
) -> Result<Json<Vec<Branch>>, AppError> {
    let branches = BranchManager::list_branches(&state.db, account_id, store_id).await?;
    Ok(Json(branches))
}

async fn get_branch(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim { account_id, .. }: SessionClaim,
    Path((store_id, branch_id)): Path<(Uuid, Uuid)>,
) -> Result<Json<Branch>, AppError> {
    let branch = BranchManager::get_branch(&state.db, account_id, store_id, branch_id).await?;
    Ok(Json(branch))
}

async fn update_branch(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim { account_id, .. }: SessionClaim,
    Path((store_id, branch_id)): Path<(Uuid, Uuid)>,
    Json(req): Json<UpdateBranchRequest>,
) -> Result<Json<Branch>, AppError> {
    let branch =
        BranchManager::update_branch(&state.db, account_id, store_id, branch_id, req).await?;
    Ok(Json(branch))
}

async fn set_working_hours(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim { account_id, .. }: SessionClaim,
    Path((store_id, branch_id)): Path<(Uuid, Uuid)>,
    Json(req): Json<SetWorkingHoursRequest>,
) -> Result<Json<Vec<BranchWorkingHours>>, AppError> {
    let hours =
        BranchManager::set_working_hours(&state.db, account_id, store_id, branch_id, req).await?;
    Ok(Json(hours))
}

async fn adjust_stock(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim { account_id, .. }: SessionClaim,
    Path((store_id, branch_id)): Path<(Uuid, Uuid)>,
    Json(req): Json<AdjustStockRequest>,
) -> Result<Json<BranchProduct>, AppError> {
    let bp =
        BranchManager::adjust_stock(&state.db, account_id, store_id, branch_id, req).await?;
    Ok(Json(bp))
}

===== backend/src/branches/service.rs
// backend/src/branches/service.rs

//! Branch service per D-062.

use crate::error::{AppError, AppResult};
use sqlx::PgPool;
use uuid::Uuid;

pub struct BranchManager;

impl BranchManager {
    /// Create branch per D-062.
    pub async fn create_branch(
        db: &PgPool,
        store_id: Uuid,
        name: &str,
        address: Option<&str>,
        phone_number: Option<&str>,
        pickup_enabled: bool,
    ) -> AppResult<Uuid> {
        let branch_id: Uuid = sqlx::query_scalar(
            r#"
            INSERT INTO app.branch (store_id, name, address, phone_number, pickup_enabled)
            VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5)
            RETURNING id
            "#,
        )
        .bind(store_id)
        .bind(name)
        .bind(address)
        .bind(phone_number)
        .bind(pickup_enabled)
        .fetch_one(db)
        .await?;

        Ok(branch_id)
    }

    /// Set working hours per D-062.
    pub async fn set_working_hours(
        db: &PgPool,
        branch_id: Uuid,
        day_of_week: i16,
        opens_at: &str,
        closes_at: &str,
    ) -> AppResult<()> {
        // Parse times (HH:MM format)
        let open_time: chrono::NaiveTime = chrono::NaiveTime::parse_from_str(opens_at, "%H:%M")
            .map_err(|_| AppError::BadRequest("Invalid time format".to_string()))?;

        let close_time: chrono::NaiveTime = chrono::NaiveTime::parse_from_str(closes_at, "%H:%M")
            .map_err(|_| AppError::BadRequest("Invalid time format".to_string()))?;

        sqlx::query(
            r#"
            INSERT INTO app.branch_working_hours (branch_id, day_of_week, opens_at, closes_at)
            VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4)
            ON CONFLICT (branch_id, day_of_week) DO UPDATE
            SET opens_at = $3, closes_at = $4
            "#,
        )
        .bind(branch_id)
        .bind(day_of_week)
        .bind(open_time)
        .bind(close_time)
        .execute(db)
        .await?;

        Ok(())
    }

    /// Get branch by ID.
    pub async fn get_branch(db: &PgPool, branch_id: Uuid) -> AppResult<BranchRow> {
        sqlx::query_as::<_, BranchRow>(
            r#"
            SELECT id, store_id, name, address, phone_number, is_published, pickup_enabled, created_at, updated_at
            FROM app.branch
            WHERE id = $1 AND deleted_at IS NULL
            "#,
        )
        .bind(branch_id)
        .fetch_optional(db)
        .await?
        .ok_or_else(|| AppError::NotFound("Branch not found".to_string()))
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, sqlx::FromRow)]
pub struct BranchRow {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub store_id: Uuid,
    pub name: String,
    pub address: Option<String>,
    pub phone_number: Option<String>,
    pub is_published: bool,
    pub pickup_enabled: bool,
    pub created_at: chrono::DateTime<chrono::Utc>,
    pub updated_at: chrono::DateTime<chrono::Utc>,
}

===== backend/src/cart/manager.rs
use crate::error::{AppError, ErrorCode};
use crate::state::AppState;
use redis::AsyncCommands;
use uuid::Uuid;
use chrono::Utc;

use super::models::*;

pub struct CartManager;

impl CartManager {
    const CART_TTL_SECONDS: u32 = 30 * 24 * 60 * 60; // 30 days per D-115

    /// Get cart key for consumer or guest session
    fn get_cart_key(store_id: Uuid, consumer_id_or_session: &str) -> String {
        format!("cart:{}:{}", store_id, consumer_id_or_session)
    }

    /// Get consumer's cart from Valkey per D-115
    pub async fn get_cart(
        cache: &mut redis::aio::ConnectionManager,
        store_id: Uuid,
        consumer_id: &str,
    ) -> Result<Cart, AppError> {
        let key = Self::get_cart_key(store_id, consumer_id);
        
        let cart_json: Option<String> = cache
            .get(&key)
            .await
            .map_err(|e| AppError::new(
                ErrorCode::CacheError,
                &format!("Valkey error: {}", e),
            ))?;

        if let Some(json) = cart_json {
            let cart: Cart = serde_json::from_str(&json).map_err(|e| {
                AppError::new(ErrorCode::InternalError, &format!("JSON parse error: {}", e))
            })?;
            Ok(cart)
        } else {
            // Empty cart
            Ok(Cart {
                store_id,
                lines: vec![],
                updated_at: Utc::now(),
            })
        }
    }

    /// Add item to cart per D-115
    pub async fn add_to_cart(
        cache: &mut redis::aio::ConnectionManager,
        store_id: Uuid,
        consumer_id: &str,
        req: AddToCartRequest,
    ) -> Result<Cart, AppError> {
        if req.quantity <= 0 {
            return Err(AppError::new(
                ErrorCode::InvalidInput,
                "Quantity must be positive",
            ));
        }

        let key = Self::get_cart_key(store_id, consumer_id);
        let mut cart = Self::get_cart(cache, store_id, consumer_id).await?;

        // Find or create line
        let line = cart.lines.iter_mut().find(|l| {
            l.product_id == req.product_id
                && l.variant_id == req.variant_id
                && l.branch_id == req.branch_id
        });

        if let Some(line) = line {
            line.quantity += req.quantity;
        } else {
            cart.lines.push(CartLine {
                product_id: req.product_id,
                variant_id: req.variant_id,
                branch_id: req.branch_id,
                quantity: req.quantity,
                unit_price_egp: 0, // Will be set by checkout validation
                added_at: Utc::now(),
            });
        }

        cart.updated_at = Utc::now();

        // Persist to Valkey with TTL
        let json = serde_json::to_string(&cart).map_err(|e| {
            AppError::new(ErrorCode::InternalError, &format!("JSON error: {}", e))
        })?;

        cache
            .set_ex(&key, json, Self::CART_TTL_SECONDS as usize)
            .await
            .map_err(|e| AppError::new(
                ErrorCode::CacheError,
                &format!("Valkey error: {}", e),
            ))?;

        Ok(cart)
    }

    /// Remove item from cart
    pub async fn remove_from_cart(
        cache: &mut redis::aio::ConnectionManager,
        store_id: Uuid,
        consumer_id: &str,
        req: RemoveFromCartRequest,
    ) -> Result<Cart, AppError> {
        let key = Self::get_cart_key(store_id, consumer_id);
        let mut cart = Self::get_cart(cache, store_id, consumer_id).await?;

        cart.lines.retain(|l| {
            !(l.product_id == req.product_id
                && l.variant_id == req.variant_id
                && l.branch_id == req.branch_id)
        });

        cart.updated_at = Utc::now();

        let json = serde_json::to_string(&cart).map_err(|e| {
            AppError::new(ErrorCode::InternalError, &format!("JSON error: {}", e))
        })?;

        cache
            .set_ex(&key, json, Self::CART_TTL_SECONDS as usize)
            .await
            .map_err(|e| AppError::new(
                ErrorCode::CacheError,
                &format!("Valkey error: {}", e),
            ))?;

        Ok(cart)
    }

    /// Merge guest cart into account cart per D-115
    pub async fn merge_guest_cart(
        cache: &mut redis::aio::ConnectionManager,
        store_id: Uuid,
        guest_session_id: &str,
        consumer_id: &str,
    ) -> Result<Cart, AppError> {
        let guest_cart = Self::get_cart(cache, store_id, guest_session_id).await?;
        let mut account_cart = Self::get_cart(cache, store_id, consumer_id).await?;

        // Merge: account values win on conflicts, non-conflicting guest lines append
        for guest_line in guest_cart.lines {
            let existing = account_cart.lines.iter_mut().find(|l| {
                l.product_id == guest_line.product_id
                    && l.variant_id == guest_line.variant_id
                    && l.branch_id == guest_line.branch_id
            });

            if existing.is_none() {
                account_cart.lines.push(guest_line);
            }
        }

        account_cart.updated_at = Utc::now();

        // Save merged cart to account key
        let key = Self::get_cart_key(store_id, consumer_id);
        let json = serde_json::to_string(&account_cart).map_err(|e| {
            AppError::new(ErrorCode::InternalError, &format!("JSON error: {}", e))
        })?;

        cache
            .set_ex(&key, json, Self::CART_TTL_SECONDS as usize)
            .await
            .map_err(|e| AppError::new(
                ErrorCode::CacheError,
                &format!("Valkey error: {}", e),
            ))?;

        // Clear guest cart
        let guest_key = Self::get_cart_key(store_id, guest_session_id);
        let _: () = cache.del(&guest_key).await.ok();

        Ok(account_cart)
    }

    /// Clear cart
    pub async fn clear_cart(
        cache: &mut redis::aio::ConnectionManager,
        store_id: Uuid,
        consumer_id: &str,
    ) -> Result<(), AppError> {
        let key = Self::get_cart_key(store_id, consumer_id);
        cache.del(&key).await.map_err(|e| {
            AppError::new(
                ErrorCode::CacheError,
                &format!("Valkey error: {}", e),
            )
        })?;
        Ok(())
    }
}

===== backend/src/cart/models.rs
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use uuid::Uuid;
use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct CartLine {
    pub product_id: Uuid,
    pub variant_id: Option<Uuid>,
    pub branch_id: Uuid,
    pub quantity: i64,
    pub unit_price_egp: i64, // Current price in piastres
    pub added_at: DateTime<Utc>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Cart {
    pub store_id: Uuid,
    pub lines: Vec<CartLine>,
    pub updated_at: DateTime<Utc>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
pub struct AddToCartRequest {
    pub product_id: Uuid,
    pub variant_id: Option<Uuid>,
    pub branch_id: Uuid,
    pub quantity: i64,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
pub struct UpdateCartLineRequest {
    pub product_id: Uuid,
    pub variant_id: Option<Uuid>,
    pub branch_id: Uuid,
    pub quantity: i64,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
pub struct RemoveFromCartRequest {
    pub product_id: Uuid,
    pub variant_id: Option<Uuid>,
    pub branch_id: Uuid,
}

#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct CartResponse {
    pub lines: Vec<CartLineResponse>,
    pub line_count: i32,
}

#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct CartLineResponse {
    pub product_id: String,
    pub variant_id: Option<String>,
    pub branch_id: String,
    pub quantity: i64,
    pub unit_price_egp: i64,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct CartValidationError {
    pub product_id: Uuid,
    pub variant_id: Option<Uuid>,
    pub error: String,
}

===== backend/src/cart/routes.rs
use axum::{
    extract::{Path, State},
    http::StatusCode,
    routing::{delete, get, post, put},
    Json, Router,
};
use uuid::Uuid;

use crate::auth::SessionClaim;
use crate::error::AppError;
use crate::state::AppState;

use super::manager::CartManager;
use super::models::*;

pub fn cart_routes() -> Router<AppState> {
    Router::new()
        .route("/stores/:store_id/cart", get(get_cart).post(add_to_cart))
        .route("/stores/:store_id/cart/remove", delete(remove_from_cart))
        .route("/stores/:store_id/cart/clear", post(clear_cart))
}

async fn get_cart(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim {
        account_id,
        consumer_id,
        ..
    }: SessionClaim,
    Path(store_id): Path<Uuid>,
) -> Result<Json<Cart>, AppError> {
    // For guest: use session ID; for account: use consumer_id
    let consumer_key = consumer_id
        .unwrap_or_else(|| account_id.to_string());

    let mut cache = state
        .cache
        .get_connection()
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::new(
            crate::error::ErrorCode::CacheError,
            &format!("Connection error: {}", e),
        ))?;

    let cart = CartManager::get_cart(&mut cache, store_id, &consumer_key).await?;
    Ok(Json(cart))
}

async fn add_to_cart(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim {
        consumer_id,
        account_id,
        ..
    }: SessionClaim,
    Path(store_id): Path<Uuid>,
    Json(req): Json<AddToCartRequest>,
) -> Result<Json<Cart>, AppError> {
    let consumer_key = consumer_id
        .unwrap_or_else(|| account_id.to_string());

    let mut cache = state
        .cache
        .get_connection()
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::new(
            crate::error::ErrorCode::CacheError,
            &format!("Connection error: {}", e),
        ))?;

    let cart = CartManager::add_to_cart(&mut cache, store_id, &consumer_key, req).await?;
    Ok(Json(cart))
}

async fn remove_from_cart(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim {
        consumer_id,
        account_id,
        ..
    }: SessionClaim,
    Path(store_id): Path<Uuid>,
    Json(req): Json<RemoveFromCartRequest>,
) -> Result<StatusCode, AppError> {
    let consumer_key = consumer_id
        .unwrap_or_else(|| account_id.to_string());

    let mut cache = state
        .cache
        .get_connection()
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::new(
            crate::error::ErrorCode::CacheError,
            &format!("Connection error: {}", e),
        ))?;

    CartManager::remove_from_cart(&mut cache, store_id, &consumer_key, req).await?;
    Ok(StatusCode::OK)
}

async fn clear_cart(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim {
        consumer_id,
        account_id,
        ..
    }: SessionClaim,
    Path(store_id): Path<Uuid>,
) -> Result<StatusCode, AppError> {
    let consumer_key = consumer_id
        .unwrap_or_else(|| account_id.to_string());

    let mut cache = state
        .cache
        .get_connection()
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::new(
            crate::error::ErrorCode::CacheError,
            &format!("Connection error: {}", e),
        ))?;

    CartManager::clear_cart(&mut cache, store_id, &consumer_key).await?;
    Ok(StatusCode::OK)
}

===== backend/src/catalog/manager.rs
use crate::error::{AppError, ErrorCode};
use crate::audit::AuditLogger;
use uuid::Uuid;
use sqlx::PgPool;
use chrono::Utc;

use super::models::*;

pub struct CatalogManager;

impl CatalogManager {
    /// Create category.
    pub async fn create_category(
        pool: &PgPool,
        account_id: Uuid,
        store_id: Uuid,
        req: CreateCategoryRequest,
    ) -> Result<Category, AppError> {
        // Verify store ownership
        let _: (Uuid,) = sqlx::query_as(
            "SELECT id FROM app.store WHERE id = $1 AND account_id = $2 AND deleted_at IS NULL",
        )
        .bind(&store_id)
        .bind(&account_id)
        .fetch_optional(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?
        .ok_or_else(|| AppError::new(ErrorCode::NotFound, "Store not found"))?;

        let slug = slugify(&req.name);
        let id = Uuid::new_v4();
        let now = Utc::now();

        sqlx::query(
            r#"
            INSERT INTO app.category (id, store_id, name, slug, description, parent_id, is_published, created_at, updated_at)
            VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, false, $7, $7)
            "#,
        )
        .bind(&id)
        .bind(&store_id)
        .bind(&req.name)
        .bind(&slug)
        .bind(&req.description)
        .bind(&req.parent_id)
        .bind(&now)
        .execute(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        AuditLogger::log_action(
            pool,
            account_id,
            "category.create",
            Some(id),
            serde_json::json!(&req),
            None,
        )
        .await?;

        Self::get_category(pool, account_id, store_id, id).await
    }

    /// Create product.
    pub async fn create_product(
        pool: &PgPool,
        account_id: Uuid,
        store_id: Uuid,
        req: CreateProductRequest,
    ) -> Result<Product, AppError> {
        // Verify store ownership
        let _: (Uuid,) = sqlx::query_as(
            "SELECT id FROM app.store WHERE id = $1 AND account_id = $2 AND deleted_at IS NULL",
        )
        .bind(&store_id)
        .bind(&account_id)
        .fetch_optional(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?
        .ok_or_else(|| AppError::new(ErrorCode::NotFound, "Store not found"))?;

        if req.base_price_egp < 0 {
            return Err(AppError::new(
                ErrorCode::InvalidInput,
                "Price cannot be negative",
            ));
        }

        let slug = slugify(&req.name);
        let id = Uuid::new_v4();
        let now = Utc::now();

        sqlx::query(
            r#"
            INSERT INTO app.product (id, store_id, name, slug, description, sku, base_price_egp, is_published, created_at, updated_at)
            VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, $7, false, $8, $8)
            "#,
        )
        .bind(&id)
        .bind(&store_id)
        .bind(&req.name)
        .bind(&slug)
        .bind(&req.description)
        .bind(&req.sku)
        .bind(req.base_price_egp)
        .bind(&now)
        .execute(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        AuditLogger::log_action(
            pool,
            account_id,
            "product.create",
            Some(id),
            serde_json::json!(&req),
            None,
        )
        .await?;

        Self::get_product(pool, account_id, store_id, id).await
    }

    /// Get product.
    pub async fn get_product(
        pool: &PgPool,
        account_id: Uuid,
        store_id: Uuid,
        product_id: Uuid,
    ) -> Result<Product, AppError> {
        // Verify store ownership
        let _: (Uuid,) = sqlx::query_as(
            "SELECT id FROM app.store WHERE id = $1 AND account_id = $2 AND deleted_at IS NULL",
        )
        .bind(&store_id)
        .bind(&account_id)
        .fetch_optional(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?
        .ok_or_else(|| AppError::new(ErrorCode::NotFound, "Store not found"))?;

        sqlx::query_as::<_, Product>(
            "SELECT * FROM app.product WHERE id = $1 AND store_id = $2 AND deleted_at IS NULL",
        )
        .bind(&product_id)
        .bind(&store_id)
        .fetch_optional(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?
        .ok_or_else(|| AppError::new(ErrorCode::NotFound, "Product not found"))
    }

    /// List products for store.
    pub async fn list_products(
        pool: &PgPool,
        account_id: Uuid,
        store_id: Uuid,
    ) -> Result<Vec<Product>, AppError> {
        // Verify store ownership
        let _: (Uuid,) = sqlx::query_as(
            "SELECT id FROM app.store WHERE id = $1 AND account_id = $2 AND deleted_at IS NULL",
        )
        .bind(&store_id)
        .bind(&account_id)
        .fetch_optional(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?
        .ok_or_else(|| AppError::new(ErrorCode::NotFound, "Store not found"))?;

        sqlx::query_as::<_, Product>(
            "SELECT * FROM app.product WHERE store_id = $1 AND deleted_at IS NULL ORDER BY name",
        )
        .bind(&store_id)
        .fetch_all(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))
    }

    /// Create variant.
    pub async fn create_variant(
        pool: &PgPool,
        account_id: Uuid,
        store_id: Uuid,
        product_id: Uuid,
        req: CreateVariantRequest,
    ) -> Result<Variant, AppError> {
        // Verify product ownership
        let _product = Self::get_product(pool, account_id, store_id, product_id).await?;

        if let Some(price) = req.price_override_egp {
            if price < 0 {
                return Err(AppError::new(
                    ErrorCode::InvalidInput,
                    "Price cannot be negative",
                ));
            }
        }

        let id = Uuid::new_v4();
        let now = Utc::now();

        sqlx::query(
            r#"
            INSERT INTO app.variant (id, product_id, name, sku, price_override_egp, created_at, updated_at)
            VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, $6)
            "#,
        )
        .bind(&id)
        .bind(&product_id)
        .bind(&req.name)
        .bind(&req.sku)
        .bind(req.price_override_egp)
        .bind(&now)
        .execute(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        AuditLogger::log_action(
            pool,
            account_id,
            "variant.create",
            Some(id),
            serde_json::json!(&req),
            None,
        )
        .await?;

        Self::get_variant(pool, account_id, store_id, product_id, id).await
    }

    /// Get variant.
    pub async fn get_variant(
        pool: &PgPool,
        account_id: Uuid,
        store_id: Uuid,
        product_id: Uuid,
        variant_id: Uuid,
    ) -> Result<Variant, AppError> {
        // Verify product ownership
        let _product = Self::get_product(pool, account_id, store_id, product_id).await?;

        sqlx::query_as::<_, Variant>(
            "SELECT * FROM app.variant WHERE id = $1 AND product_id = $2",
        )
        .bind(&variant_id)
        .bind(&product_id)
        .fetch_optional(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?
        .ok_or_else(|| AppError::new(ErrorCode::NotFound, "Variant not found"))
    }

    /// Publish product.
    pub async fn publish_product(
        pool: &PgPool,
        account_id: Uuid,
        store_id: Uuid,
        product_id: Uuid,
        publish: bool,
    ) -> Result<Product, AppError> {
        let product = Self::get_product(pool, account_id, store_id, product_id).await?;
        let now = Utc::now();

        sqlx::query(
            "UPDATE app.product SET is_published = $1, updated_at = $2 WHERE id = $3",
        )
        .bind(publish)
        .bind(&now)
        .bind(&product_id)
        .execute(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        AuditLogger::log_action(
            pool,
            account_id,
            if publish { "product.publish" } else { "product.unpublish" },
            Some(product_id),
            serde_json::json!({}),
            None,
        )
        .await?;

        Self::get_product(pool, account_id, store_id, product_id).await
    }

    /// Add product image.
    pub async fn add_product_image(
        pool: &PgPool,
        account_id: Uuid,
        store_id: Uuid,
        product_id: Uuid,
        req: AddProductImageRequest,
    ) -> Result<ProductImage, AppError> {
        // Verify product ownership
        let _product = Self::get_product(pool, account_id, store_id, product_id).await?;

        // Verify media ownership (belongs to store)
        let _: (Uuid,) = sqlx::query_as(
            "SELECT id FROM app.media WHERE id = $1 AND store_id = $2",
        )
        .bind(&req.media_id)
        .bind(&store_id)
        .fetch_optional(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?
        .ok_or_else(|| AppError::new(ErrorCode::NotFound, "Media not found"))?;

        let id = Uuid::new_v4();
        let now = Utc::now();

        // Get next sort order
        let (sort_order,): (i32,) = sqlx::query_as(
            "SELECT COALESCE(MAX(sort_order), -1) + 1 FROM app.product_image WHERE product_id = $1",
        )
        .bind(&product_id)
        .fetch_one(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        sqlx::query(
            "INSERT INTO app.product_image (id, product_id, media_id, sort_order, created_at) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5)"
        )
        .bind(&id)
        .bind(&product_id)
        .bind(&req.media_id)
        .bind(sort_order)
        .bind(&now)
        .execute(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        Ok(ProductImage {
            id,
            product_id,
            media_id: req.media_id,
            sort_order,
            created_at: now,
        })
    }
}

/// Simple slug generation: lowercase, remove special chars, replace spaces with hyphens.
fn slugify(s: &str) -> String {
    s.to_lowercase()
        .chars()
        .map(|c| {
            if c.is_alphanumeric() {
                c
            } else if c.is_whitespace() {
                '-'
            } else {
                continue;
            }
        })
        .collect::<String>()
        .split('-')
        .filter(|p| !p.is_empty())
        .collect::<Vec<_>>()
        .join("-")
}

/// Get category
impl CatalogManager {
    pub async fn get_category(
        pool: &PgPool,
        account_id: Uuid,
        store_id: Uuid,
        category_id: Uuid,
    ) -> Result<Category, AppError> {
        // Verify store ownership
        let _: (Uuid,) = sqlx::query_as(
            "SELECT id FROM app.store WHERE id = $1 AND account_id = $2 AND deleted_at IS NULL",
        )
        .bind(&store_id)
        .bind(&account_id)
        .fetch_optional(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?
        .ok_or_else(|| AppError::new(ErrorCode::NotFound, "Store not found"))?;

        sqlx::query_as::<_, Category>(
            "SELECT * FROM app.category WHERE id = $1 AND store_id = $2",
        )
        .bind(&category_id)
        .bind(&store_id)
        .fetch_optional(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?
        .ok_or_else(|| AppError::new(ErrorCode::NotFound, "Category not found"))
    }
}

===== backend/src/catalog/models.rs
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use uuid::Uuid;
use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, sqlx::FromRow)]
pub struct Category {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub store_id: Uuid,
    pub name: String,
    pub slug: String,
    pub description: Option<String>,
    pub parent_id: Option<Uuid>,
    pub is_published: bool,
    pub created_at: DateTime<Utc>,
    pub updated_at: DateTime<Utc>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, sqlx::FromRow)]
pub struct Product {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub store_id: Uuid,
    pub name: String,
    pub slug: String,
    pub description: Option<String>,
    pub sku: Option<String>,
    pub base_price_egp: i64, // in piastres
    pub is_published: bool,
    pub created_at: DateTime<Utc>,
    pub updated_at: DateTime<Utc>,
    pub deleted_at: Option<DateTime<Utc>>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, sqlx::FromRow)]
pub struct Variant {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub product_id: Uuid,
    pub name: String,
    pub sku: Option<String>,
    pub price_override_egp: Option<i64>, // NULL = use base price
    pub created_at: DateTime<Utc>,
    pub updated_at: DateTime<Utc>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, sqlx::FromRow)]
pub struct ProductImage {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub product_id: Uuid,
    pub media_id: Uuid,
    pub sort_order: i32,
    pub created_at: DateTime<Utc>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
pub struct CreateCategoryRequest {
    pub name: String,
    pub description: Option<String>,
    pub parent_id: Option<Uuid>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
pub struct UpdateCategoryRequest {
    pub name: Option<String>,
    pub description: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
pub struct CreateProductRequest {
    pub name: String,
    pub description: Option<String>,
    pub sku: Option<String>,
    pub base_price_egp: i64,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
pub struct UpdateProductRequest {
    pub name: Option<String>,
    pub description: Option<String>,
    pub sku: Option<String>,
    pub base_price_egp: Option<i64>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
pub struct CreateVariantRequest {
    pub name: String,
    pub sku: Option<String>,
    pub price_override_egp: Option<i64>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
pub struct AddProductImageRequest {
    pub media_id: Uuid,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
pub struct PublishProductRequest {
    pub publish: bool,
}
