===== backend/src/catalog/routes.rs
use axum::{
    extract::{Path, State},
    http::StatusCode,
    routing::{get, post, put},
    Json, Router,
};
use uuid::Uuid;

use crate::auth::SessionClaim;
use crate::error::AppError;
use crate::state::AppState;

use super::manager::CatalogManager;
use super::models::*;

pub fn catalog_routes() -> Router<AppState> {
    Router::new()
        .route(
            "/stores/:store_id/categories",
            post(create_category).get(list_categories),
        )
        .route(
            "/stores/:store_id/categories/:category_id",
            get(get_category).put(update_category),
        )
        .route(
            "/stores/:store_id/products",
            post(create_product).get(list_products),
        )
        .route(
            "/stores/:store_id/products/:product_id",
            get(get_product).put(update_product),
        )
        .route(
            "/stores/:store_id/products/:product_id/publish",
            post(publish_product),
        )
        .route(
            "/stores/:store_id/products/:product_id/variants",
            post(create_variant).get(list_variants),
        )
        .route(
            "/stores/:store_id/products/:product_id/images",
            post(add_product_image),
        )
}

async fn create_category(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim { account_id, .. }: SessionClaim,
    Path(store_id): Path<Uuid>,
    Json(req): Json<CreateCategoryRequest>,
) -> Result<(StatusCode, Json<Category>), AppError> {
    let category = CatalogManager::create_category(&state.db, account_id, store_id, req).await?;
    Ok((StatusCode::CREATED, Json(category)))
}

async fn get_category(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim { account_id, .. }: SessionClaim,
    Path((store_id, category_id)): Path<(Uuid, Uuid)>,
) -> Result<Json<Category>, AppError> {
    let category =
        CatalogManager::get_category(&state.db, account_id, store_id, category_id).await?;
    Ok(Json(category))
}

async fn list_categories(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim { account_id, .. }: SessionClaim,
    Path(store_id): Path<Uuid>,
) -> Result<Json<Vec<Category>>, AppError> {
    let categories: Vec<Category> = sqlx::query_as(
        "SELECT * FROM app.category WHERE store_id = $1 ORDER BY name",
    )
    .bind(&store_id)
    .fetch_all(&state.db)
    .await
    .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

    Ok(Json(categories))
}

async fn update_category(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim { account_id, .. }: SessionClaim,
    Path((store_id, category_id)): Path<(Uuid, Uuid)>,
    Json(req): Json<UpdateCategoryRequest>,
) -> Result<Json<Category>, AppError> {
    // Verify ownership first
    let _category =
        CatalogManager::get_category(&state.db, account_id, store_id, category_id).await?;

    sqlx::query(
        "UPDATE app.category SET name = COALESCE($1, name), description = COALESCE($2, description) WHERE id = $3",
    )
    .bind(&req.name)
    .bind(&req.description)
    .bind(&category_id)
    .execute(&state.db)
    .await
    .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

    CatalogManager::get_category(&state.db, account_id, store_id, category_id).await
}

async fn create_product(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim { account_id, .. }: SessionClaim,
    Path(store_id): Path<Uuid>,
    Json(req): Json<CreateProductRequest>,
) -> Result<(StatusCode, Json<Product>), AppError> {
    let product = CatalogManager::create_product(&state.db, account_id, store_id, req).await?;
    Ok((StatusCode::CREATED, Json(product)))
}

async fn get_product(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim { account_id, .. }: SessionClaim,
    Path((store_id, product_id)): Path<(Uuid, Uuid)>,
) -> Result<Json<Product>, AppError> {
    let product = CatalogManager::get_product(&state.db, account_id, store_id, product_id).await?;
    Ok(Json(product))
}

async fn list_products(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim { account_id, .. }: SessionClaim,
    Path(store_id): Path<Uuid>,
) -> Result<Json<Vec<Product>>, AppError> {
    let products = CatalogManager::list_products(&state.db, account_id, store_id).await?;
    Ok(Json(products))
}

async fn update_product(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim { account_id, .. }: SessionClaim,
    Path((store_id, product_id)): Path<(Uuid, Uuid)>,
    Json(req): Json<UpdateProductRequest>,
) -> Result<Json<Product>, AppError> {
    let _product = CatalogManager::get_product(&state.db, account_id, store_id, product_id).await?;

    sqlx::query(
        "UPDATE app.product SET name = COALESCE($1, name), description = COALESCE($2, description), sku = COALESCE($3, sku), base_price_egp = COALESCE($4, base_price_egp) WHERE id = $5",
    )
    .bind(&req.name)
    .bind(&req.description)
    .bind(&req.sku)
    .bind(req.base_price_egp)
    .bind(&product_id)
    .execute(&state.db)
    .await
    .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

    CatalogManager::get_product(&state.db, account_id, store_id, product_id).await
}

async fn publish_product(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim { account_id, .. }: SessionClaim,
    Path((store_id, product_id)): Path<(Uuid, Uuid)>,
    Json(req): Json<PublishProductRequest>,
) -> Result<Json<Product>, AppError> {
    let product =
        CatalogManager::publish_product(&state.db, account_id, store_id, product_id, req.publish)
            .await?;
    Ok(Json(product))
}

async fn create_variant(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim { account_id, .. }: SessionClaim,
    Path((store_id, product_id)): Path<(Uuid, Uuid)>,
    Json(req): Json<CreateVariantRequest>,
) -> Result<(StatusCode, Json<Variant>), AppError> {
    let variant =
        CatalogManager::create_variant(&state.db, account_id, store_id, product_id, req)
            .await?;
    Ok((StatusCode::CREATED, Json(variant)))
}

async fn list_variants(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim { account_id, .. }: SessionClaim,
    Path((store_id, product_id)): Path<(Uuid, Uuid)>,
) -> Result<Json<Vec<Variant>>, AppError> {
    let _product = CatalogManager::get_product(&state.db, account_id, store_id, product_id).await?;

    let variants: Vec<Variant> =
        sqlx::query_as("SELECT * FROM app.variant WHERE product_id = $1 ORDER BY name")
            .bind(&product_id)
            .fetch_all(&state.db)
            .await
            .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

    Ok(Json(variants))
}

async fn add_product_image(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim { account_id, .. }: SessionClaim,
    Path((store_id, product_id)): Path<(Uuid, Uuid)>,
    Json(req): Json<AddProductImageRequest>,
) -> Result<(StatusCode, Json<ProductImage>), AppError> {
    let image =
        CatalogManager::add_product_image(&state.db, account_id, store_id, product_id, req)
            .await?;
    Ok((StatusCode::CREATED, Json(image)))
}

===== backend/src/catalog/service.rs
// backend/src/catalog/service.rs

//! Catalog service per D-063.

use crate::error::{AppError, AppResult};
use sqlx::PgPool;
use uuid::Uuid;

pub struct CatalogManager;

impl CatalogManager {
    /// Create category per D-063.
    pub async fn create_category(
        db: &PgPool,
        store_id: Uuid,
        name: &str,
        slug: &str,
        description: Option<&str>,
    ) -> AppResult<Uuid> {
        let category_id: Uuid = sqlx::query_scalar(
            r#"
            INSERT INTO app.category (store_id, name, slug, description)
            VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4)
            RETURNING id
            "#,
        )
        .bind(store_id)
        .bind(name)
        .bind(slug)
        .bind(description)
        .fetch_one(db)
        .await?;

        Ok(category_id)
    }

    /// Create product per D-063.
    pub async fn create_product(
        db: &PgPool,
        store_id: Uuid,
        sku: Option<&str>,
        name: &str,
        description: Option<&str>,
        category_id: Option<Uuid>,
        base_price_egp: i64,
    ) -> AppResult<Uuid> {
        if base_price_egp < 0 {
            return Err(AppError::BadRequest("Price must be non-negative".to_string()));
        }

        let product_id: Uuid = sqlx::query_scalar(
            r#"
            INSERT INTO app.product (store_id, sku, name, description, category_id, base_price_egp)
            VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6)
            RETURNING id
            "#,
        )
        .bind(store_id)
        .bind(sku)
        .bind(name)
        .bind(description)
        .bind(category_id)
        .bind(base_price_egp)
        .fetch_one(db)
        .await?;

        Ok(product_id)
    }

    /// Create variant per D-063.
    pub async fn create_variant(
        db: &PgPool,
        product_id: Uuid,
        name: &str,
        sku: Option<&str>,
        price_override_egp: Option<i64>,
    ) -> AppResult<Uuid> {
        if let Some(price) = price_override_egp {
            if price < 0 {
                return Err(AppError::BadRequest("Price must be non-negative".to_string()));
            }
        }

        let variant_id: Uuid = sqlx::query_scalar(
            r#"
            INSERT INTO app.variant (product_id, name, sku, price_override_egp)
            VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4)
            RETURNING id
            "#,
        )
        .bind(product_id)
        .bind(name)
        .bind(sku)
        .bind(price_override_egp)
        .fetch_one(db)
        .await?;

        Ok(variant_id)
    }

    /// Get product by ID.
    pub async fn get_product(db: &PgPool, product_id: Uuid) -> AppResult<ProductRow> {
        sqlx::query_as::<_, ProductRow>(
            r#"
            SELECT id, store_id, sku, name, description, category_id, base_price_egp,
                   is_published, created_at, updated_at
            FROM app.product
            WHERE id = $1 AND deleted_at IS NULL
            "#,
        )
        .bind(product_id)
        .fetch_optional(db)
        .await?
        .ok_or_else(|| AppError::NotFound("Product not found".to_string()))
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, sqlx::FromRow)]
pub struct ProductRow {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub store_id: Uuid,
    pub sku: Option<String>,
    pub name: String,
    pub description: Option<String>,
    pub category_id: Option<Uuid>,
    pub base_price_egp: i64,
    pub is_published: bool,
    pub created_at: chrono::DateTime<chrono::Utc>,
    pub updated_at: chrono::DateTime<chrono::Utc>,
}

===== backend/src/checkout/manager.rs
use crate::error::{AppError, ErrorCode};
use crate::cart::{Cart, CartManager};
use crate::shipping::ShippingManager;
use sqlx::PgPool;
use uuid::Uuid;

use super::models::*;

pub struct CheckoutManager;

impl CheckoutManager {
    /// Validate cart before checkout per D-114, D-115, D-119
    pub async fn validate_cart(
        db: &PgPool,
        store_id: Uuid,
        cart: &Cart,
        delivery_governorate: &str,
        delivery_county: &str,
        delivery_district: &str,
        branch_id_for_pickup: Option<Uuid>,
    ) -> Result<CheckoutCartValidation, AppError> {
        let mut errors = vec![];
        let mut warnings = vec![];

        if cart.lines.is_empty() {
            return Err(AppError::new(
                ErrorCode::InvalidState,
                "Cart is empty",
            ));
        }

        // Check each line for availability and pricing
        for line in &cart.lines {
            // Verify product exists and is published
            let product: Option<(bool, i64)> = sqlx::query_as(
                "SELECT is_published, base_price_egp FROM app.product WHERE id = $1 AND store_id = $2 AND deleted_at IS NULL",
            )
            .bind(&line.product_id)
            .bind(&store_id)
            .fetch_optional(db)
            .await
            .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

            let (is_published, base_price) = match product {
                Some((published, price)) if published => (published, price),
                _ => {
                    errors.push(CheckoutValidationError {
                        product_id: line.product_id.to_string(),
                        reason: "Product not available".to_string(),
                    });
                    continue;
                }
            };

            // Check branch product stock
            let stock: Option<(i64,)> = sqlx::query_as(
                "SELECT stock_qty FROM app.branch_product WHERE branch_id = $1 AND product_id = $2",
            )
            .bind(&line.branch_id)
            .bind(&line.product_id)
            .fetch_optional(db)
            .await
            .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

            if let Some((qty,)) = stock {
                if qty < line.quantity {
                    errors.push(CheckoutValidationError {
                        product_id: line.product_id.to_string(),
                        reason: format!("Only {} in stock", qty),
                    });
                }
            } else {
                errors.push(CheckoutValidationError {
                    product_id: line.product_id.to_string(),
                    reason: "Not available at selected branch".to_string(),
                });
            }
        }

        // Check shipping availability per D-119
        if branch_id_for_pickup.is_none() {
            let shipping_rule = ShippingManager::find_matching_rule(
                db,
                store_id,
                None,
                delivery_governorate,
                delivery_county,
                delivery_district,
            )
            .await?;

            if shipping_rule.is_none() {
                errors.push(CheckoutValidationError {
                    product_id: "shipping".to_string(),
                    reason: "Shipping not available to this address".to_string(),
                });
            }
        }

        Ok(CheckoutCartValidation {
            is_valid: errors.is_empty(),
            errors,
            warnings,
        })
    }

    /// Calculate checkout summary with tax, shipping, promotions per D-053, D-114, D-118
    pub async fn calculate_checkout(
        db: &PgPool,
        store_id: Uuid,
        cart: &Cart,
        delivery_governorate: &str,
        delivery_county: &str,
        delivery_district: &str,
        branch_pickup_id: Option<Uuid>,
        coupon_code: Option<&str>,
    ) -> Result<CheckoutSummary, AppError> {
        let mut subtotal_egp: i64 = 0;
        let mut line_items = vec![];

        // Fetch current prices and compute subtotal
        for line in &cart.lines {
            let (product_name, base_price): (String, i64) = sqlx::query_as(
                "SELECT name, base_price_egp FROM app.product WHERE id = $1 AND store_id = $2",
            )
            .bind(&line.product_id)
            .bind(&store_id)
            .fetch_one(db)
            .await
            .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

            let unit_price = if let Some(variant_id) = line.variant_id {
                let override_price: Option<(Option<i64>,)> = sqlx::query_as(
                    "SELECT price_override_egp FROM app.variant WHERE id = $1 AND product_id = $2",
                )
                .bind(&variant_id)
                .bind(&line.product_id)
                .fetch_optional(db)
                .await
                .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

                override_price
                    .and_then(|(p,)| p)
                    .unwrap_or(base_price)
            } else {
                base_price
            };

            let line_total = unit_price * line.quantity;
            subtotal_egp += line_total;

            let variant_name: Option<String> = if let Some(variant_id) = line.variant_id {
                sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT name FROM app.variant WHERE id = $1")
                    .bind(&variant_id)
                    .fetch_optional(db)
                    .await
                    .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?
            } else {
                None
            };

            line_items.push(CheckoutLineItem {
                product_id: line.product_id.to_string(),
                product_name,
                variant_id: line.variant_id.map(|v| v.to_string()),
                variant_name,
                quantity: line.quantity,
                unit_price_egp: unit_price,
                line_total_egp: line_total,
                line_tax_egp: 0, // Will be calculated per line
            });
        }

        // Get tax rate per D-118 (tax-exclusive per D-053)
        let tax_rate_bp: i32 = sqlx::query_scalar(
            "SELECT tax_rate_bp FROM app.store_settings WHERE store_id = $1",
        )
        .bind(&store_id)
        .fetch_one(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        // Calculate tax per line
        let mut total_tax_egp: i64 = 0;
        for item in &mut line_items {
            let line_tax = (item.line_total_egp * tax_rate_bp as i64) / 10000;
            item.line_tax_egp = line_tax;
            total_tax_egp += line_tax;
        }

        // Get shipping cost per D-119
        let shipping_cost_egp = if let Some(branch_id) = branch_pickup_id {
            0 // Pickup is free
        } else {
            let rule = ShippingManager::find_matching_rule(
                db,
                store_id,
                None,
                delivery_governorate,
                delivery_county,
                delivery_district,
            )
            .await?
            .ok_or_else(|| AppError::new(
                ErrorCode::InvalidInput,
                "Shipping not available to this address",
            ))?;

            rule.cost_egp
        };

        // Apply coupon if provided (TODO: promotion application logic)
        let mut discount_egp: i64 = 0;
        if let Some(code) = coupon_code {
            // TODO: Look up coupon, verify validity, apply discount
        }

        // Calculate total with rounding per D-053 (round to nearest EGP at final total)
        let subtotal_with_tax = subtotal_egp + total_tax_egp;
        let total_before_rounding = subtotal_with_tax + shipping_cost_egp - discount_egp;
        let total_egp = ((total_before_rounding + 50) / 100) * 100; // Round to nearest EGP
        let rounding_delta_egp = total_egp - total_before_rounding;

        Ok(CheckoutSummary {
            line_items,
            subtotal_egp,
            discount_egp,
            tax_egp: total_tax_egp,
            shipping_cost_egp,
            total_egp,
            rounding_delta_egp,
        })
    }
}

===== backend/src/checkout/routes.rs
use axum::{
    extract::{Path, State},
    http::StatusCode,
    routing::{get, post},
    Json, Router,
};
use uuid::Uuid;

use crate::auth::SessionClaim;
use crate::cart::CartManager;
use crate::error::AppError;
use crate::state::AppState;

use super::manager::CheckoutManager;
use super::models::*;

pub fn checkout_routes() -> Router<AppState> {
    Router::new()
        .route(
            "/stores/:store_id/checkout/validate",
            post(validate_checkout),
        )
        .route(
            "/stores/:store_id/checkout/summary",
            post(get_checkout_summary),
        )
}

async fn validate_checkout(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim {
        consumer_id,
        account_id,
        ..
    }: SessionClaim,
    Path(store_id): Path<Uuid>,
    Json(req): Json<CheckoutRequest>,
) -> Result<Json<CheckoutCartValidation>, AppError> {
    let consumer_key = consumer_id
        .unwrap_or_else(|| account_id.to_string());

    let mut cache = state
        .cache
        .get_connection()
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::new(
            crate::error::ErrorCode::CacheError,
            &format!("Connection error: {}", e),
        ))?;

    let cart = CartManager::get_cart(&mut cache, store_id, &consumer_key).await?;

    let validation = CheckoutManager::validate_cart(
        &state.db,
        store_id,
        &cart,
        &req.delivery_governorate,
        &req.delivery_county,
        &req.delivery_district,
        req.branch_pickup_id,
    )
    .await?;

    Ok(Json(validation))
}

async fn get_checkout_summary(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim {
        consumer_id,
        account_id,
        ..
    }: SessionClaim,
    Path(store_id): Path<Uuid>,
    Json(req): Json<CheckoutRequest>,
) -> Result<Json<CheckoutSummary>, AppError> {
    let consumer_key = consumer_id
        .unwrap_or_else(|| account_id.to_string());

    let mut cache = state
        .cache
        .get_connection()
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::new(
            crate::error::ErrorCode::CacheError,
            &format!("Connection error: {}", e),
        ))?;

    let cart = CartManager::get_cart(&mut cache, store_id, &consumer_key).await?;

    let summary = CheckoutManager::calculate_checkout(
        &state.db,
        store_id,
        &cart,
        &req.delivery_governorate,
        &req.delivery_county,
        &req.delivery_district,
        req.branch_pickup_id,
        req.coupon_code.as_deref(),
    )
    .await?;

    Ok(Json(summary))
}

===== backend/src/consumer_auth/login.rs
// backend/src/consumer_auth/login.rs

//! Consumer login per D-109: email + password, store-scoped sessions.

use crate::error::{AppError, AppResult};
use crate::auth::password::PasswordManager;
use crate::auth::session::{SessionClaim, SessionManager};
use sqlx::PgPool;
use redis::aio::ConnectionManager;
use uuid::Uuid;

pub struct ConsumerLoginManager;

impl ConsumerLoginManager {
    /// Authenticate consumer per D-109: email + password per store.
    pub async fn login(
        db: &PgPool,
        store_id: Uuid,
        email: &str,
        password: &str,
    ) -> AppResult<Uuid> {
        let normalized_email = email.to_lowercase();

        // Lookup consumer by store_id + normalized_email
        let consumer: Option<(Uuid, String)> = sqlx::query_as(
            r#"
            SELECT id, password_hash
            FROM app.consumer
            WHERE store_id = $1 AND normalized_email = $2 AND deleted_at IS NULL
            "#,
        )
        .bind(store_id)
        .bind(&normalized_email)
        .fetch_optional(db)
        .await?;

        let (consumer_id, password_hash) = consumer.ok_or_else(|| {
            // Generic error to avoid disclosing existence
            AppError::AuthenticationFailed
        })?;

        // Verify password
        let valid = PasswordManager::verify_password(password, &password_hash)?;
        if !valid {
            return Err(AppError::AuthenticationFailed);
        }

        Ok(consumer_id)
    }

    /// Issue consumer session per D-109.
    /// Secure, HttpOnly, SameSite=Lax, 30-day absolute TTL, 7-day idle TTL.
    pub async fn issue_session(
        cache: &ConnectionManager,
        store_id: Uuid,
        consumer_id: Uuid,
    ) -> AppResult<String> {
        let session_id = Uuid::new_v4();
        let token = uuid::Uuid::new_v4().to_string();

        let claim = SessionClaim {
            session_id,
            account_id: consumer_id, // Reuse for simplicity
            context_id: store_id,
            role: "consumer".to_string(),
            permission_version: 1,
            issued_at: chrono::Utc::now(),
            expires_at: chrono::Utc::now() + chrono::Duration::days(30),
        };

        let key = format!("consumer_session:{}", token);
        let mut conn = cache.clone();

        redis::cmd("SET")
            .arg(&key)
            .arg(serde_json::to_string(&claim)?)
            .arg("EX")
            .arg(30 * 24 * 60 * 60) // 30 days
            .query_async::<_, ()>(&mut conn)
            .await?;

        Ok(token)
    }

    /// Verify consumer session.
    pub async fn verify_session(
        cache: &ConnectionManager,
        token: &str,
    ) -> AppResult<SessionClaim> {
        let key = format!("consumer_session:{}", token);
        let mut conn = cache.clone();

        let data: Option<String> = redis::cmd("GET")
            .arg(&key)
            .query_async(&mut conn)
            .await?;

        match data {
            Some(json) => {
                let claim: SessionClaim = serde_json::from_str(&json)?;

                if chrono::Utc::now() > claim.expires_at {
                    redis::cmd("DEL")
                        .arg(&key)
                        .query_async::<_, ()>(&mut conn)
                        .await?;
                    return Err(AppError::SessionExpired);
                }

                Ok(claim)
            }
            None => Err(AppError::SessionExpired),
        }
    }
}

===== backend/src/consumer_auth/reset.rs
// backend/src/consumer_auth/reset.rs

//! Consumer password reset per D-128: email or WhatsApp OTP, 1-hour TTL.

use crate::error::{AppError, AppResult};
use crate::auth::password::PasswordManager;
use sqlx::PgPool;
use uuid::Uuid;

pub struct ConsumerPasswordResetManager;

impl ConsumerPasswordResetManager {
    /// Initiate password reset per D-128.
    /// Look up by store_id + email without disclosing existence.
    /// Send email or WhatsApp OTP depending on consumer preference.
    pub async fn initiate_reset(
        db: &PgPool,
        store_id: Uuid,
        email: &str,
    ) -> AppResult<()> {
        let normalized_email = email.to_lowercase();

        // Lookup without disclosing existence
        let consumer: Option<(Uuid, bool, Option<String>)> = sqlx::query_as(
            r#"
            SELECT id, whatsapp_verified_at IS NOT NULL, phone_number
            FROM app.consumer
            WHERE store_id = $1 AND normalized_email = $2 AND deleted_at IS NULL
            "#,
        )
        .bind(store_id)
        .bind(&normalized_email)
        .fetch_optional(db)
        .await?;

        match consumer {
            Some((consumer_id, whatsapp_verified, phone_number)) => {
                if whatsapp_verified && phone_number.is_some() {
                    // Send WhatsApp OTP
                    super::signup::ConsumerSignupManager::send_whatsapp_otp(
                        phone_number.as_ref().unwrap(),
                        consumer_id,
                    )
                    .await?;
                } else {
                    // Send email reset link
                    send_password_reset_email(consumer_id, &normalized_email).await?;
                }
            }
            None => {
                // Generic response (no disclosure)
                tracing::warn!(
                    "Password reset requested for non-existent consumer: {}",
                    normalized_email
                );
            }
        }

        Ok(())
    }

    /// Verify reset token and change password per D-128.
    pub async fn reset_password(
        db: &PgPool,
        consumer_id: Uuid,
        new_password: &str,
        _token: &str,
    ) -> AppResult<()> {
        // TODO: Verify reset token signature
        // TODO: Check 1-hour TTL

        // Validate password per D-111
        PasswordManager::validate_password_policy(new_password)?;

        // Check HIBP
        let is_breached = PasswordManager::check_hibp_breach(new_password)
            .await
            .unwrap_or(false);
        if is_breached {
            return Err(AppError::BadRequest(
                "Password is compromised. Please choose another.".to_string(),
            ));
        }

        // Hash and update
        let password_hash = PasswordManager::hash_password(new_password)?;

        sqlx::query(
            r#"
            UPDATE app.consumer
            SET password_hash = $1, updated_at = now()
            WHERE id = $2
            "#,
        )
        .bind(&password_hash)
        .bind(consumer_id)
        .execute(db)
        .await?;

        // TODO: Invalidate all existing sessions per D-128

        Ok(())
    }
}

async fn send_password_reset_email(consumer_id: Uuid, email: &str) -> AppResult<()> {
    // TODO: Generate signed reset link (1-hour TTL)
    // TODO: Send SES email
    tracing::info!("Password reset email sent to {}", email);
    Ok(())
}

===== backend/src/consumer_auth/routes.rs
// backend/src/consumer_auth/routes.rs

//! Consumer authentication routes.

use crate::{
    consumer_auth::{
        login::ConsumerLoginManager,
        models::{ConsumerSignupRequest, ConsumerSignupResponse, ConsumerLoginRequest, ConsumerLoginResponse},
        signup::ConsumerSignupManager,
    },
    error::AppResult,
    state::AppState,
};
use axum::{
    extract::{Path, State},
    http::StatusCode,
    Json,
};

pub fn router() -> axum::Router<AppState> {
    axum::Router::new()
        .route("/signup", axum::routing::post(signup))
        .route("/verify", axum::routing::post(verify))
        .route("/:store_slug/login", axum::routing::post(login))
}

/// POST /api/v1/consumer/signup
async fn signup(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    Json(input): Json<ConsumerSignupRequest>,
) -> AppResult<(StatusCode, Json<ConsumerSignupResponse>)> {
    // TODO: Extract store_id from context or request
    let store_id = uuid::Uuid::nil(); // Placeholder

    let (_consumer_id, verification_method) = ConsumerSignupManager::signup(
        &state.db,
        store_id,
        &input.email,
        &input.password,
        &input.name,
        input.phone_number.as_deref(),
    )
    .await?;

    // Send verification based on method
    ConsumerSignupManager::send_email_verification(store_id, uuid::Uuid::nil(), &input.email)
        .await?;

    Ok((
        StatusCode::CREATED,
        Json(ConsumerSignupResponse {
            email_verified: false,
            verification_sent_via: verification_method,
        }),
    ))
}

/// POST /api/v1/consumer/verify

) -> AppResult<StatusCode> {

}

/// POST /api/v1/consumer/:store_slug/login
async fn login(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    Path(_store_slug): Path<String>,
    Json(input): Json<ConsumerLoginRequest>,
) -> AppResult<(StatusCode, Json<ConsumerLoginResponse>)> {
    // TODO: Resolve store_slug to store_id
    let store_id = uuid::Uuid::nil(); // Placeholder

    let consumer_id = ConsumerLoginManager::login(&state.db, store_id, &input.email, &input.password).await?;

    let session_token = ConsumerLoginManager::issue_session(&state.cache, store_id, consumer_id).await?;

    Ok((
        StatusCode::OK,
        Json(ConsumerLoginResponse { session_token }),
    ))
}

===== backend/src/consumer_auth/signup.rs
// backend/src/consumer_auth/signup.rs

//! Consumer signup per D-131: email verification or WhatsApp OTP per D-128.

use crate::error::{AppError, AppResult};
use crate::auth::password::PasswordManager;
use sqlx::PgPool;
use uuid::Uuid;

pub struct ConsumerSignupManager;

impl ConsumerSignupManager {
    /// Signup: create consumer with pending email verification per D-131.
    /// Check phone on WhatsApp if provided per D-128.
    /// Rate limits: 5 signup per IP per hour, 3 resends per email per hour per D-131.
    pub async fn signup(
        db: &PgPool,
        store_id: Uuid,
        email: &str,
        password: &str,
        name: &str,
        phone_number: Option<&str>,
    ) -> AppResult<(Uuid, String)> {
        // Validate password per D-111
        PasswordManager::validate_password_policy(password)?;

        // Check for HIBP breach per D-111
        let is_breached = PasswordManager::check_hibp_breach(password).await.unwrap_or(false);
        if is_breached {
            return Err(AppError::BadRequest(
                "Password is compromised. Please choose another.".to_string(),
            ));
        }

        // Normalize email
        let normalized_email = email.to_lowercase();

        // Check uniqueness within store
        let exists: bool = sqlx::query_scalar(
            r#"
            SELECT EXISTS(
                SELECT 1 FROM app.consumer
                WHERE store_id = $1 AND normalized_email = $2 AND deleted_at IS NULL
            )
            "#,
        )
        .bind(store_id)
        .bind(&normalized_email)
        .fetch_one(db)
        .await?;

        if exists {
            // Return generic message without disclosing existence per D-131
            return Err(AppError::BadRequest(
                "Account exists or verification required".to_string(),
            ));
        }

        // Hash password
        let password_hash = PasswordManager::hash_password(password)?;

        // Determine verification method per D-128
        let verification_method = if let Some(phone) = phone_number {
            // Check if phone is on WhatsApp
            let on_whatsapp = check_phone_on_whatsapp(phone).await.unwrap_or(false);
            if on_whatsapp {
                "whatsapp_otp"
            } else {
                "email_only"
            }
        } else {
            "email_only"
        };

        // Create consumer
        let consumer_id: Uuid = sqlx::query_scalar(
            r#"
            INSERT INTO app.consumer
            (store_id, normalized_email, password_hash, phone_number, full_name)
            VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5)
            RETURNING id
            "#,
        )
        .bind(store_id)
        .bind(&normalized_email)
        .bind(&password_hash)
        .bind(phone_number)
        .bind(name)
        .fetch_one(db)
        .await?;

        Ok((consumer_id, verification_method.to_string()))
    }

    /// Send email verification link per D-131.
    /// One-hour TTL, single-use token.
    pub async fn send_email_verification(
        store_id: Uuid,
        consumer_id: Uuid,
        email: &str,
    ) -> AppResult<()> {
        // TODO: Generate signed token (store_id, consumer_id, email_hash, timestamp)
        // TODO: Send SES email with verification link
        tracing::info!("Email verification sent to {}", email);
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Send WhatsApp OTP per D-128.
    /// Rate limit: 5 per hour per phone per D-128.
    pub async fn send_whatsapp_otp(
        phone_number: &str,
        consumer_id: Uuid,
    ) -> AppResult<()> {
        // TODO: Check rate limit per phone
        // TODO: Generate 6-digit OTP
        // TODO: Call WhatsApp Business API
        tracing::info!("WhatsApp OTP sent to {}", phone_number);
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Verify email link per D-131.
    pub async fn verify_email_link(
        db: &PgPool,
        consumer_id: Uuid,
        token: &str,
    ) -> AppResult<()> {
        // TODO: Verify signed token
        // TODO: Update consumer.email_verified_at
        // TODO: Issue session

        sqlx::query(
            r#"
            UPDATE app.consumer
            SET email_verified_at = now()
            WHERE id = $1
            "#,
        )
        .bind(consumer_id)
        .execute(db)
        .await?;

        Ok(())
    }

    /// Verify WhatsApp OTP per D-128.
    pub async fn verify_whatsapp_otp(
        db: &PgPool,
        consumer_id: Uuid,
        code: &str,
    ) -> AppResult<()> {
        // TODO: Check OTP against Valkey cache
        // TODO: Mark as verified
        // TODO: Burn OTP (one-time use)

        sqlx::query(
            r#"
            UPDATE app.consumer
            SET whatsapp_verified_at = now()
            WHERE id = $1
            "#,
        )
        .bind(consumer_id)
        .execute(db)
        .await?;

        Ok(())
    }
}

===== backend/src/consumer/manager.rs
use crate::error::{AppError, ErrorCode};
use uuid::Uuid;
use sqlx::PgPool;
use chrono::Utc;

use super::models::*;

pub struct ConsumerManager;

impl ConsumerManager {
    /// Get or create consumer account (store-scoped per D-021)
    pub async fn get_or_create_consumer(
        db: &PgPool,
        store_id: Uuid,
        email: &str,
        name: &str,
    ) -> Result<ConsumerAccount, AppError> {
        // Normalize email
        let normalized_email = email.to_lowercase();

        // Try to get existing
        let existing: Option<ConsumerAccount> = sqlx::query_as(
            "SELECT * FROM app.consumer WHERE store_id = $1 AND email = $2 AND deleted_at IS NULL",
        )
        .bind(&store_id)
        .bind(&normalized_email)
        .fetch_optional(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        if let Some(consumer) = existing {
            return Ok(consumer);
        }

        // Create new consumer
        let id = Uuid::new_v4();
        let now = Utc::now();

        sqlx::query(
            r#"
            INSERT INTO app.consumer (id, store_id, email, name, created_at, updated_at)
            VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $5)
            "#,
        )
        .bind(&id)
        .bind(&store_id)
        .bind(&normalized_email)
        .bind(name)
        .bind(&now)
        .execute(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        // Create default consent
        sqlx::query(
            "INSERT INTO app.consumer_consent (consumer_id, marketing_emails, whatsapp_messages, analytics, third_party, updated_at) VALUES ($1, false, false, false, false, $2)",
        )
        .bind(&id)
        .bind(&now)
        .execute(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        Self::get_consumer(db, store_id, id).await
    }

    /// Get consumer account
    pub async fn get_consumer(
        db: &PgPool,
        store_id: Uuid,
        consumer_id: Uuid,
    ) -> Result<ConsumerAccount, AppError> {
        sqlx::query_as::<_, ConsumerAccount>(
            "SELECT * FROM app.consumer WHERE id = $1 AND store_id = $2 AND deleted_at IS NULL",
        )
        .bind(&consumer_id)
        .bind(&store_id)
        .fetch_optional(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?
        .ok_or_else(|| AppError::new(ErrorCode::NotFound, "Consumer not found"))
    }

    /// Update profile
    pub async fn update_profile(
        db: &PgPool,
        store_id: Uuid,
        consumer_id: Uuid,
        req: UpdateProfileRequest,
    ) -> Result<ConsumerAccount, AppError> {
        let _consumer = Self::get_consumer(db, store_id, consumer_id).await?;

        let now = Utc::now();

        sqlx::query(
            "UPDATE app.consumer SET name = COALESCE($1, name), phone = COALESCE($2, phone), updated_at = $3 WHERE id = $4",
        )
        .bind(&req.name)
        .bind(&req.phone)
        .bind(&now)
        .bind(&consumer_id)
        .execute(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        Self::get_consumer(db, store_id, consumer_id).await
    }

    /// Add address
    pub async fn add_address(
        db: &PgPool,
        store_id: Uuid,
        consumer_id: Uuid,
        req: AddAddressRequest,
    ) -> Result<ConsumerAddress, AppError> {
        let _consumer = Self::get_consumer(db, store_id, consumer_id).await?;

        let id = Uuid::new_v4();

        sqlx::query(
            "INSERT INTO app.consumer_address (id, consumer_id, governorate, county, district, address, phone) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, $7)",
        )
        .bind(&id)
        .bind(&consumer_id)
        .bind(&req.governorate)
        .bind(&req.county)
        .bind(&req.district)
        .bind(&req.address)
        .bind(&req.phone)
        .execute(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        sqlx::query_as::<_, ConsumerAddress>(
            "SELECT * FROM app.consumer_address WHERE id = $1",
        )
        .bind(&id)
        .fetch_one(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))
    }

    /// Update consent
    pub async fn update_consent(
        db: &PgPool,
        store_id: Uuid,
        consumer_id: Uuid,
        req: UpdateConsentRequest,
    ) -> Result<ConsumerConsent, AppError> {
        let _consumer = Self::get_consumer(db, store_id, consumer_id).await?;

        let now = Utc::now();

        sqlx::query(
            r#"
            UPDATE app.consumer_consent
            SET marketing_emails = COALESCE($1, marketing_emails),
                whatsapp_messages = COALESCE($2, whatsapp_messages),
                analytics = COALESCE($3, analytics),
                third_party = COALESCE($4, third_party),
                updated_at = $5
            WHERE consumer_id = $6
            "#,
        )
        .bind(req.marketing_emails)
        .bind(req.whatsapp_messages)
        .bind(req.analytics)
        .bind(req.third_party)
        .bind(&now)
        .bind(&consumer_id)
        .execute(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        sqlx::query_as::<_, ConsumerConsent>(
            "SELECT * FROM app.consumer_consent WHERE consumer_id = $1",
        )
        .bind(&consumer_id)
        .fetch_one(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))
    }

    /// Soft delete consumer per D-010 (30-day recovery window)
    pub async fn request_deletion(
        db: &PgPool,
        store_id: Uuid,
        consumer_id: Uuid,
    ) -> Result<(), AppError> {
        let now = Utc::now();
        let recovery_deadline = now + chrono::Duration::days(30);

        sqlx::query(
            "UPDATE app.consumer SET deleted_at = $1 WHERE id = $2 AND store_id = $3",
        )
        .bind(&now)
        .bind(&consumer_id)
        .bind(&store_id)
        .execute(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        // TODO: Schedule anonymization after 30 days

        Ok(())
    }
}

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use uuid::Uuid;
use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, sqlx::FromRow)]
pub struct ConsumerAccount {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub store_id: Uuid,
    pub email: String,
    pub name: String,
    pub phone: Option<String>,
    pub created_at: DateTime<Utc>,
    pub updated_at: DateTime<Utc>,
    pub deleted_at: Option<DateTime<Utc>>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, sqlx::FromRow)]
pub struct ConsumerAddress {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub consumer_id: Uuid,
    pub governorate: String,
    pub county: String,
    pub district: String,
    pub address: String,
    pub phone: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, sqlx::FromRow)]
pub struct ConsumerConsent {
    pub consumer_id: Uuid,
    pub marketing_emails: bool,
    pub whatsapp_messages: bool,
    pub analytics: bool,
    pub third_party: bool,
    pub updated_at: DateTime<Utc>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
pub struct UpdateProfileRequest {
    pub name: Option<String>,
    pub phone: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
pub struct AddAddressRequest {
    pub governorate: String,
    pub county: String,
    pub district: String,
    pub address: String,
    pub phone: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
pub struct UpdateConsentRequest {
    pub marketing_emails: Option<bool>,
    pub whatsapp_messages: Option<bool>,
    pub analytics: Option<bool>,
    pub third_party: Option<bool>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct ConsumerProfileResponse {
    pub id: String,
    pub email: String,
    pub name: String,
    pub phone: Option<String>,
    pub addresses: Vec<ConsumerAddress>,
    pub consent: ConsumerConsent,
}

===== backend/src/consumer/routes.rs
use axum::{
    extract::{Path, State},
    http::StatusCode,
    routing::{get, post, put},
    Json, Router,
};
use uuid::Uuid;

use crate::auth::SessionClaim;
use crate::error::AppError;
use crate::state::AppState;

use super::manager::ConsumerManager;
use super::models::*;

pub fn consumer_routes() -> Router<AppState> {
    Router::new()
        .route("/stores/:store_id/consumer/profile", get(get_profile).put(update_profile))
        .route("/stores/:store_id/consumer/addresses", post(add_address).get(list_addresses))
        .route("/stores/:store_id/consumer/consent", put(update_consent))
        .route("/stores/:store_id/consumer/delete", post(request_deletion))
}

async fn get_profile(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim { consumer_id, .. }: SessionClaim,
    Path(store_id): Path<Uuid>,
) -> Result<Json<ConsumerProfileResponse>, AppError> {
    let consumer = ConsumerManager::get_consumer(&state.db, store_id, consumer_id).await?;

    let addresses: Vec<ConsumerAddress> = sqlx::query_as(
        "SELECT * FROM app.consumer_address WHERE consumer_id = $1",
    )
    .bind(&consumer_id)
    .fetch_all(&state.db)
    .await
    .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

    let consent: ConsumerConsent = sqlx::query_as(
        "SELECT * FROM app.consumer_consent WHERE consumer_id = $1",
    )
    .bind(&consumer_id)
    .fetch_one(&state.db)
    .await
    .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

    Ok(Json(ConsumerProfileResponse {
        id: consumer.id.to_string(),
        email: consumer.email,
        name: consumer.name,
        phone: consumer.phone,
        addresses,
        consent,
    }))
}

async fn update_profile(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim { consumer_id, .. }: SessionClaim,
    Path(store_id): Path<Uuid>,
    Json(req): Json<UpdateProfileRequest>,
) -> Result<Json<ConsumerAccount>, AppError> {
    let consumer = ConsumerManager::update_profile(&state.db, store_id, consumer_id, req).await?;
    Ok(Json(consumer))
}

async fn add_address(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim { consumer_id, .. }: SessionClaim,
    Path(store_id): Path<Uuid>,
    Json(req): Json<AddAddressRequest>,
) -> Result<(StatusCode, Json<ConsumerAddress>), AppError> {
    let address = ConsumerManager::add_address(&state.db, store_id, consumer_id, req).await?;
    Ok((StatusCode::CREATED, Json(address)))
}

async fn list_addresses(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim { consumer_id, .. }: SessionClaim,
) -> Result<Json<Vec<ConsumerAddress>>, AppError> {
    let addresses: Vec<ConsumerAddress> = sqlx::query_as(
        "SELECT * FROM app.consumer_address WHERE consumer_id = $1",
    )
    .bind(&consumer_id)
    .fetch_all(&state.db)
    .await
    .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

    Ok(Json(addresses))
}

async fn update_consent(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim { consumer_id, .. }: SessionClaim,
    Path(store_id): Path<Uuid>,
    Json(req): Json<UpdateConsentRequest>,
) -> Result<Json<ConsumerConsent>, AppError> {
    let consent = ConsumerManager::update_consent(&state.db, store_id, consumer_id, req).await?;
    Ok(Json(consent))
}

async fn request_deletion(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim { consumer_id, .. }: SessionClaim,
    Path(store_id): Path<Uuid>,
) -> Result<StatusCode, AppError> {
    ConsumerManager::request_deletion(&state.db, store_id, consumer_id).await?;
    Ok(StatusCode::OK)
}

===== backend/src/csrf/mod.rs
// backend/src/csrf/mod.rs

//! CSRF protection per D-110: double-submit token on consumer (SameSite=Lax) and admin (SameSite=Strict).

use crate::error::{AppError, AppResult};
use axum::{
    async_trait,
    extract::FromRequestParts,
    http::request::Parts,
};
use uuid::Uuid;

/// CSRF token extractor from X-CSRF-Token header.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct CsrfToken(pub String);

#[async_trait]
impl FromRequestParts<crate::state::AppState> for CsrfToken {
    type Rejection = AppError;

    async fn from_request_parts(
        parts: &mut Parts,
        _state: &crate::state::AppState,
    ) -> Result<Self, Self::Rejection> {
        parts
            .headers
            .get("x-csrf-token")
            .and_then(|h| h.to_str().ok())
            .map(|s| CsrfToken(s.to_string()))
            .ok_or_else(|| AppError::BadRequest("X-CSRF-Token header required".to_string()))
    }
}

/// Generate new CSRF token.
pub fn generate_csrf_token() -> String {
    Uuid::new_v4().to_string()
}

/// Verify CSRF token from session per D-110.
pub async fn verify_csrf_token(
    cache: &redis::aio::ConnectionManager,
    session_token: &str,
    csrf_token: &str,
) -> AppResult<()> {
    // TODO: Verify token matches session's stored token
    // TODO: Rotate on next session update
    Ok(())
}

===== backend/src/delegation/approval_request.rs
// backend/src/delegation/approval_request.rs

//! Approval request creation and decision per D-026.

use crate::error::{AppError, AppResult};
use sha2::{Sha256, Digest};
use sqlx::PgPool;
use uuid::Uuid;

pub struct ApprovalRequestManager;

impl ApprovalRequestManager {
    /// Create approval request for dual-control action per D-026.
    /// Payload is hashed for mutation detection.
    pub async fn create_request(
        db: &PgPool,
        account_id: Uuid,
        context_id: Uuid,
        action_type: &str,
        action_payload: &serde_json::Value,
    ) -> AppResult<Uuid> {
        // Compute SHA256 hash of payload
        let payload_json = serde_json::to_string(action_payload)?;
        let mut hasher = Sha256::new();
        hasher.update(payload_json.as_bytes());
        let payload_hash = format!("{:x}", hasher.finalize());

        // Calculate expiry (10 minutes per D-026)
        let expires_at = chrono::Utc::now() + chrono::Duration::minutes(10);

        let request_id: Uuid = sqlx::query_scalar(
            r#"
            INSERT INTO app.approval_request
            (account_id, context_id, action_type, action_payload, payload_hash, expires_at)
            VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6)
            RETURNING id
            "#,
        )
        .bind(account_id)
        .bind(context_id)
        .bind(action_type)
        .bind(action_payload)
        .bind(&payload_hash)
        .bind(expires_at)
        .fetch_one(db)
        .await?;

        Ok(request_id)
    }

    /// Approve an approval request per D-026.
    pub async fn approve(
        db: &PgPool,
        request_id: Uuid,
        approved_by_id: Uuid,
    ) -> AppResult<()> {
        sqlx::query(
            r#"
            UPDATE app.approval_request
            SET state = 'approved', approved_by_id = $1, decided_at = now()
            WHERE id = $2 AND state = 'pending'
            "#,
        )
        .bind(approved_by_id)
        .bind(request_id)
        .execute(db)
        .await?;

        Ok(())
    }

    /// Reject an approval request.
    pub async fn reject(
        db: &PgPool,
        request_id: Uuid,
        rejected_by_id: Uuid,
        reason: &str,
    ) -> AppResult<()> {
        sqlx::query(
            r#"
            UPDATE app.approval_request
            SET state = 'rejected', approved_by_id = $1, rejected_reason = $2, decided_at = now()
            WHERE id = $3 AND state = 'pending'
            "#,
        )
        .bind(rejected_by_id)
        .bind(reason)
        .bind(request_id)
        .execute(db)
        .await?;

        Ok(())
    }

    /// Get approval request and check state.
    pub async fn get(
        db: &PgPool,
        request_id: Uuid,
    ) -> AppResult<ApprovalRequestRow> {
        sqlx::query_as::<_, ApprovalRequestRow>(
            r#"
            SELECT id, account_id, context_id, action_type, action_payload, payload_hash, state,
                   approved_by_id, decided_at, expires_at, created_at
            FROM app.approval_request
            WHERE id = $1
            "#,
        )
        .bind(request_id)
        .fetch_optional(db)
        .await?
        .ok_or_else(|| AppError::NotFound("Approval request not found".to_string()))
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, sqlx::FromRow)]
pub struct ApprovalRequestRow {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub account_id: Uuid,
    pub context_id: Uuid,
    pub action_type: String,
    pub action_payload: serde_json::Value,
    pub payload_hash: String,
    pub state: String,
    pub approved_by_id: Option<Uuid>,
    pub decided_at: Option<chrono::DateTime<chrono::Utc>>,
    pub expires_at: chrono::DateTime<chrono::Utc>,
    pub created_at: chrono::DateTime<chrono::Utc>,
}

// backend/src/delegation/dual_control.rs

//! Dual-control tier definitions per D-026.

pub const DUAL_CONTROL_CAPABILITIES: &[&str] = &[
    "employees.manage",
    "permissions.grant",
    "store.delete",
    "store.settings.billing",
    "store.transfer",
    "orders.refund",
    "apikeys.manage",
    "webhooks.manage",
];

pub const NON_DELEGABLE_CAPABILITIES: &[&str] = &[
    "permissions.grant",
    "employees.manage",
    "store.delete",
    "store.settings.billing",
    "store.transfer",
    "orders.refund",
    "apikeys.manage",
    "webhooks.manage",
];

pub const SUPER_ADMIN_FLOOR: &[&str] = &[
    "permissions.grant",
    "employees.manage",
    "store.delete",
    "store.transfer",
];

/// Check if capability requires dual-control approval.
pub fn requires_dual_control(capability: &str) -> bool {
    DUAL_CONTROL_CAPABILITIES.contains(&capability)
}

/// Check if capability is non-delegable (can't be granted to others).
pub fn is_non_delegable(capability: &str) -> bool {
    NON_DELEGABLE_CAPABILITIES.contains(&capability)
}

/// Check if capability is super-admin-floor (can't be delegated at all).
pub fn is_super_admin_floor(capability: &str) -> bool {
    SUPER_ADMIN_FLOOR.contains(&capability)
}

===== backend/src/delegation/execution_token.rs
// backend/src/delegation/execution_token.rs

//! Execution tokens bound to approval request and payload hash per D-026.

use crate::error::{AppError, AppResult};
use sha2::{Sha256, Digest};
use sqlx::PgPool;
use uuid::Uuid;

pub struct ExecutionTokenManager;

impl ExecutionTokenManager {
    /// Create execution token bound to approval request and payload hash per D-026.
    /// Token is single-use with 10-minute TTL.
    pub async fn create(
        db: &PgPool,
        approval_request_id: Uuid,
        account_id: Uuid,
        employee_account_id: Option<Uuid>,
        payload_hash: &str,
    ) -> AppResult<String> {
        let token = uuid::Uuid::new_v4().to_string();
        let expires_at = chrono::Utc::now() + chrono::Duration::minutes(10);

        sqlx::query(
            r#"
            INSERT INTO app.execution_token
            (approval_request_id, account_id, employee_account_id, token_hash, payload_hash, expires_at)
            VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6)
            "#,
        )
        .bind(approval_request_id)
        .bind(account_id)
        .bind(employee_account_id)
        .bind(&token) // In production, hash this
        .bind(payload_hash)
        .bind(expires_at)
        .execute(db)
        .await?;

        Ok(token)
    }

    /// Verify execution token: check hash matches payload and token is unused per D-026.
    pub async fn verify_and_consume(
        db: &PgPool,
        token: &str,
        payload: &serde_json::Value,
    ) -> AppResult<ExecutionTokenRow> {
        // Compute payload hash
        let payload_json = serde_json::to_string(payload)?;
        let mut hasher = Sha256::new();
        hasher.update(payload_json.as_bytes());
        let payload_hash = format!("{:x}", hasher.finalize());

        // Fetch token
        let token_row: ExecutionTokenRow = sqlx::query_as(
            r#"
            SELECT id, approval_request_id, account_id, employee_account_id, token_hash,
                   payload_hash, executed, expires_at
            FROM app.execution_token
            WHERE token_hash = $1 AND executed = false AND expires_at > now()
            "#,
        )
        .bind(token)
        .fetch_optional(db)
        .await?
        .ok_or_else(|| {
            AppError::InvalidToken
        })?;

        // Verify payload hash matches
        if token_row.payload_hash != payload_hash {
            return Err(AppError::Conflict(
                "Payload does not match approval request".to_string(),
            ));
        }

        // Mark as executed
        sqlx::query(
            r#"
            UPDATE app.execution_token
            SET executed = true, executed_at = now()
            WHERE id = $1
            "#,
        )
        .bind(token_row.id)
        .execute(db)
        .await?;

        Ok(token_row)
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, sqlx::FromRow)]
pub struct ExecutionTokenRow {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub approval_request_id: Uuid,
    pub account_id: Uuid,
    pub employee_account_id: Option<Uuid>,
    pub token_hash: String,
    pub payload_hash: String,
    pub executed: bool,
    pub expires_at: chrono::DateTime<chrono::Utc>,
}

===== backend/src/feedback/manager.rs
use crate::error::{AppError, ErrorCode};
use uuid::Uuid;
use sqlx::PgPool;
use chrono::Utc;

use super::models::*;

pub struct FeedbackManager;

impl FeedbackManager {
    /// Create feedback (rate limited per role)
    pub async fn create_feedback(
        db: &PgPool,
        account_id: Uuid,
        store_id: Option<Uuid>,
        req: CreateFeedbackRequest,
    ) -> Result<FeedbackItem, AppError> {
        // Rate limits per D-077
        let rate_limit = match req.category.as_str() {
            "bug" => 5, // Consumer: 5 per day
            _ => match store_id {
                Some(_) => 10, // Merchant: 10 per day
                None => 20,    // Employee: 20 per day
            },
        };

        let recent: (i64,) = sqlx::query_as(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM app.feedback_item WHERE account_id = $1 AND created_at > now() - interval '1 day'",
        )
        .bind(&account_id)
        .fetch_one(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        if recent.0 >= rate_limit {
            return Err(AppError::new(
                ErrorCode::RateLimited,
                "Daily feedback limit exceeded",
            ));
        }

        let id = Uuid::new_v4();
        let now = Utc::now();

        // Bugs are never published per D-077 (CHECK constraint)
        let is_published = req.category != "bug";

        sqlx::query(
            r#"
            INSERT INTO app.feedback_item (id, account_id, store_id, category, title, description, state, is_published, created_at, updated_at)
            VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, 'open', $7, $8, $8)
            "#,
        )
        .bind(&id)
        .bind(&account_id)
        .bind(&store_id)
        .bind(&req.category)
        .bind(&req.title)
        .bind(&req.description)
        .bind(is_published)
        .bind(&now)
        .execute(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        Self::get_feedback(db, id).await
    }

    /// Get feedback (visibility rules)
    pub async fn get_feedback(db: &PgPool, feedback_id: Uuid) -> Result<FeedbackItem, AppError> {
        sqlx::query_as::<_, FeedbackItem>(
            "SELECT * FROM app.feedback_item WHERE id = $1",
        )
        .bind(&feedback_id)
        .fetch_optional(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?
        .ok_or_else(|| AppError::new(ErrorCode::NotFound, "Feedback not found"))
    }

    /// List published feedback (admin can see all)
    pub async fn list_public_feedback(db: &PgPool) -> Result<Vec<FeedbackItem>, AppError> {
        sqlx::query_as::<_, FeedbackItem>(
            "SELECT * FROM app.feedback_item WHERE is_published = true AND category != 'bug' ORDER BY updated_at DESC",
        )
        .fetch_all(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))
    }
}

    pub category: String,        // feature, improvement, bug, util
    pub title: String,
    pub description: String,
    pub state: String, // open, under_review, planned, in_progress, shipped, declined
    pub is_published: bool,

    pub updated_at: DateTime<Utc>,
}

===== backend/src/feedback/routes.rs
use axum::{
    extract::{Path, State},
    http::StatusCode,
    routing::{get, post},
    Json, Router,
};
use uuid::Uuid;

use crate::auth::SessionClaim;
use crate::error::AppError;
use crate::state::AppState;

use super::manager::FeedbackManager;
use super::models::*;

pub fn feedback_routes() -> Router<AppState> {
    Router::new()
        .route("/feedback", post(create_feedback).get(list_feedback))
        .route("/feedback/:id", get(get_feedback))
}

async fn create_feedback(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim { account_id, .. }: SessionClaim,
    Json(req): Json<CreateFeedbackRequest>,
) -> Result<(StatusCode, Json<FeedbackResponse>), AppError> {
    let feedback = FeedbackManager::create_feedback(&state.db, account_id, None, req).await?;

    Ok((
        StatusCode::CREATED,
        Json(FeedbackResponse {
            id: feedback.id.to_string(),
            category: feedback.category,
            title: feedback.title,
            state: feedback.state,
            created_at: feedback.created_at.to_rfc3339(),
        }),
    ))
}

async fn get_feedback(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    Path(feedback_id): Path<Uuid>,
) -> Result<Json<FeedbackItem>, AppError> {
    let feedback = FeedbackManager::get_feedback(&state.db, feedback_id).await?;
    Ok(Json(feedback))
}

async fn list_feedback(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
) -> Result<Json<Vec<FeedbackItem>>, AppError> {
    let items = FeedbackManager::list_public_feedback(&state.db).await?;
    Ok(Json(items))
}

===== backend/src/guardduty/manager.rs
use crate::error::AppError;
use crate::media::MediaManager;
use sqlx::PgPool;
use uuid::Uuid;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

/// Payload from EventBridge when GuardDuty scan status tag changes
#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
pub struct GuardDutyScanStatusEvent {
    pub s3_bucket: String,
    pub s3_object: String,
    pub scan_status: String, // NO_THREATS_FOUND, THREATS_FOUND, UNSUPPORTED, ACCESS_DENIED, FAILED
    pub timestamp: String,
}

pub struct GuardDutyManager;

impl GuardDutyManager {
    /// Handle incoming EventBridge event for scan status change.
    /// Called by Lambda/worker receiving events from rabita-staging bucket.
    pub async fn handle_scan_event(
        pool: &PgPool,
        event: GuardDutyScanStatusEvent,
    ) -> Result<(), AppError> {
        // Find media record by S3 key
        let media_id: Option<(Uuid,)> = sqlx::query_as(
            "SELECT id FROM app.media WHERE s3_key_staging = $1",
        )
        .bind(&event.s3_object)
        .fetch_optional(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        let media_id = media_id
            .ok_or_else(|| AppError::new(
                crate::error::ErrorCode::NotFound,
                "Media record not found for S3 key",
            ))?
            .0;

        // Route to appropriate handler based on status
        MediaManager::on_scan_status_changed(pool, media_id, event.s3_object, &event.scan_status)
            .await?;

        Ok(())
    }
}

===== backend/src/idempotency/middleware.rs
// backend/src/idempotency/middleware.rs

//! Idempotency middleware per D-130.

use crate::error::{AppError, AppResult};
use axum::{
    async_trait,
    extract::FromRequestParts,
    http::request::Parts,
};

/// Extractor for Idempotency-Key header.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct IdempotencyKey(pub String);

#[async_trait]
impl FromRequestParts<crate::state::AppState> for IdempotencyKey {
    type Rejection = AppError;

    async fn from_request_parts(
        parts: &mut Parts,
        _state: &crate::state::AppState,
    ) -> Result<Self, Self::Rejection> {
        parts
            .headers
            .get("idempotency-key")
            .and_then(|h| h.to_str().ok())
            .map(|s| IdempotencyKey(s.to_string()))
            .ok_or_else(|| AppError::BadRequest("Idempotency-Key header required".to_string()))
    }
}

===== backend/src/idempotency/store.rs
// backend/src/idempotency/store.rs

//! Idempotency key storage per D-130: principal-scoped to prevent cross-principal replay.

use crate::error::{AppError, AppResult};
use sqlx::PgPool;
use sha2::{Sha256, Digest};

pub struct IdempotencyStore;

impl IdempotencyStore {
    /// Store idempotency key response per D-130.
    /// 24-hour TTL, keyed by (principal_type, principal_id, key).
    pub async fn store(
        db: &PgPool,
        principal_type: &str,
        principal_id: &str,
        key: &str,
        method: &str,
        path: &str,
        payload: &[u8],
        status: u16,
        response_body: &serde_json::Value,
    ) -> AppResult<()> {
        let mut hasher = Sha256::new();
        hasher.update(payload);
        let payload_hash = format!("{:x}", hasher.finalize());

        let expires_at = chrono::Utc::now() + chrono::Duration::hours(24);

        sqlx::query(
            r#"
            INSERT INTO app.idempotency_key
            (principal_type, principal_id, key, method, path, payload_hash, response_status, response_body, expires_at)
            VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, $7, $8, $9)
            ON CONFLICT (principal_type, principal_id, key) DO UPDATE
            SET response_status = $7, response_body = $8
            "#,
        )
        .bind(principal_type)
        .bind(principal_id)
        .bind(key)
        .bind(method)
        .bind(path)
        .bind(&payload_hash)
        .bind(status as i16)
        .bind(response_body)
        .bind(expires_at)
        .execute(db)
        .await?;

        Ok(())
    }

    /// Retrieve idempotency key response per D-130.
    /// Returns (status, body) if found and payload hash matches.
    /// Returns 409 Conflict if key exists but payload_hash differs (mutated payload).
    pub async fn retrieve(
        db: &PgPool,
        principal_type: &str,
        principal_id: &str,
        key: &str,
        payload: &[u8],
    ) -> AppResult<Option<(u16, serde_json::Value)>> {
        let mut hasher = Sha256::new();
        hasher.update(payload);
        let payload_hash = format!("{:x}", hasher.finalize());

        let row: Option<(i16, String, serde_json::Value)> = sqlx::query_as(
            r#"
            SELECT response_status, payload_hash, response_body
            FROM app.idempotency_key
            WHERE principal_type = $1 AND principal_id = $2 AND key = $3 AND expires_at > now()
            "#,
        )
        .bind(principal_type)
        .bind(principal_id)
        .bind(key)
        .fetch_optional(db)
        .await?;

        match row {
            Some((status, stored_hash, body)) => {
                if stored_hash != payload_hash {
                    // Payload mutated
                    return Err(AppError::Conflict(
                        "Idempotency key payload mismatch".to_string(),
                    ));
                }
                Ok(Some((status as u16, body)))
            }
            None => Ok(None),
        }
    }

    /// Check if key requires idempotency per D-130.
    pub fn requires_idempotency(method: &str, path: &str) -> bool {
        // All mutations require idempotency key per D-130
        matches!(method, "POST" | "PUT" | "PATCH" | "DELETE")
    }
}

===== backend/src/invoices/manager.rs
use crate::error::AppError;
use uuid::Uuid;
use sqlx::PgPool;

use super::models::Invoice;

pub struct InvoiceManager;

impl InvoiceManager {
    /// Generate invoice PDF per D-122
    /// Arabic-first template per D-127
    pub async fn generate_invoice_pdf(
        db: &PgPool,
        store_id: Uuid,
        order_id: Uuid,
    ) -> Result<Vec<u8>, AppError> {
        // Fetch order and store data
        let order: (String, String, i64) = sqlx::query_as(
            "SELECT public_order_number, consumer_email, (order_snapshot ->> 'total_egp')::bigint FROM app.order WHERE id = $1 AND store_id = $2",
        )
        .bind(&order_id)
        .bind(&store_id)
        .fetch_one(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        let store: (String, Option<String>) = sqlx::query_as(
            "SELECT name, logo_media_id FROM app.store WHERE id = $1",
        )
        .bind(&store_id)
        .fetch_one(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        // TODO: Use a PDF library (printpdf, tinytemplate) to generate invoice
        // For now, return stub
        let pdf_data = vec![0u8; 1024];

        Ok(pdf_data)
    }

    /// Get invoice number per D-122 (configurable format)
    pub async fn generate_invoice_number(
        db: &PgPool,
        store_id: Uuid,
    ) -> Result<String, AppError> {
        // Check counter type
        let counter_type: (String,) = sqlx::query_as(
            "SELECT counter_type FROM app.invoice_counter WHERE store_id = $1",
        )
        .bind(&store_id)
        .fetch_optional(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?
        .unwrap_or(("sequential_per_store".to_string(),));

        let store: (String,) = sqlx::query_as("SELECT slug FROM app.store WHERE id = $1")
            .bind(&store_id)
            .fetch_one(db)
            .await
            .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        // Get next number
        let next_num: (i32,) = sqlx::query_as(
            "SELECT COALESCE(MAX(CAST(REGEXP_SUBSTR(invoice_number, '[0-9]+$') AS INTEGER)), 0) + 1 FROM app.invoice WHERE store_id = $1",
        )
        .bind(&store_id)
        .fetch_one(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        Ok(format!("{}-{:06}", store.0, next_num.0))
    }
}
