===== backend/src/jobs/manager.rs
use crate::error::{AppError, ErrorCode};
use uuid::Uuid;
use sqlx::PgPool;
use chrono::Utc;

use super::models::*;

pub struct JobsManager;

impl JobsManager {
    /// Create job post (merchant only)
    pub async fn create_job_post(
        db: &PgPool,
        store_id: Uuid,
        req: CreateJobPostRequest,
    ) -> Result<JobPost, AppError> {
        let id = Uuid::new_v4();
        let now = Utc::now();

        sqlx::query(
            r#"
            INSERT INTO app.job_post (id, store_id, title, description, category, contact_phone, state, is_confidential, created_at, updated_at)
            VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, 'draft', $7, $8, $8)
            "#,
        )
        .bind(&id)
        .bind(&store_id)
        .bind(&req.title)
        .bind(&req.description)
        .bind(&req.category)
        .bind(&req.contact_phone)
        .bind(req.is_confidential.unwrap_or(false))
        .bind(&now)
        .execute(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        Self::get_job_post(db, id).await
    }

    /// Get job post
    pub async fn get_job_post(db: &PgPool, job_id: Uuid) -> Result<JobPost, AppError> {
        sqlx::query_as::<_, JobPost>(
            "SELECT * FROM app.job_post WHERE id = $1",
        )
        .bind(&job_id)
        .fetch_optional(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?
        .ok_or_else(|| AppError::new(ErrorCode::NotFound, "Job post not found"))
    }

    /// List public job posts (for public /jobs page)
    pub async fn list_public_jobs(db: &PgPool) -> Result<Vec<JobPost>, AppError> {
        sqlx::query_as::<_, JobPost>(
            "SELECT * FROM app.job_post WHERE state = 'public' ORDER BY created_at DESC",
        )
        .fetch_all(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))
    }

    /// Apply to job
    pub async fn apply_to_job(
        db: &PgPool,
        job_id: Uuid,
        req: SubmitApplicationRequest,
    ) -> Result<JobApplication, AppError> {
        let id = Uuid::new_v4();
        let now = Utc::now();

        sqlx::query(
            r#"
            INSERT INTO app.job_application (id, job_id, applicant_name, applicant_email, state, cv_media_id, created_at, updated_at)
            VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, 'applied', $5, $6, $6)
            "#,
        )
        .bind(&id)
        .bind(&job_id)
        .bind(&req.applicant_name)
        .bind(&req.applicant_email)
        .bind(&req.cv_media_id)
        .bind(&now)
        .execute(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        // Create chat thread
        let thread_id = Uuid::new_v4();
        sqlx::query(
            "INSERT INTO app.job_chat_thread (id, job_id, application_id, created_at) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4)",
        )
        .bind(&thread_id)
        .bind(&job_id)
        .bind(&id)
        .bind(&now)
        .execute(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        Self::get_application(db, id).await
    }

    /// Get application
    pub async fn get_application(db: &PgPool, app_id: Uuid) -> Result<JobApplication, AppError> {
        sqlx::query_as::<_, JobApplication>(
            "SELECT * FROM app.job_application WHERE id = $1",
        )
        .bind(&app_id)
        .fetch_optional(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?
        .ok_or_else(|| AppError::new(ErrorCode::NotFound, "Application not found"))
    }

    /// Send chat message (rate limit: 10 per minute per thread)
    pub async fn send_message(
        db: &PgPool,
        thread_id: Uuid,
        sender_id: Uuid,
        is_from_merchant: bool,
        req: SendChatMessageRequest,
    ) -> Result<JobChatMessage, AppError> {
        // Rate limit check
        let recent: (i64,) = sqlx::query_as(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM app.job_chat_message WHERE thread_id = $1 AND created_at > now() - interval '1 minute'",
        )
        .bind(&thread_id)
        .fetch_one(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        if recent.0 >= 10 {
            return Err(AppError::new(
                ErrorCode::RateLimited,
                "Message rate limit exceeded",
            ));
        }

        let id = Uuid::new_v4();
        let now = Utc::now();

        sqlx::query(
            "INSERT INTO app.job_chat_message (id, thread_id, sender_id, content, is_from_merchant, created_at) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6)",
        )
        .bind(&id)
        .bind(&thread_id)
        .bind(&sender_id)
        .bind(&req.content)
        .bind(is_from_merchant)
        .bind(&now)
        .execute(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        Ok(JobChatMessage {
            id,
            thread_id,
            sender_id,
            content: req.content,
            is_from_merchant,
            created_at: now,
        })
    }
}

===== backend/src/jobs/models.rs
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use uuid::Uuid;
use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, sqlx::FromRow)]
pub struct JobPost {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub store_id: Uuid,
    pub title: String,
    pub description: String,
    pub category: String,
    pub contact_phone: String,
    pub state: String, // draft, public, unpublished, archived
    pub is_confidential: bool, // noindex if true per D-098
    pub created_at: DateTime<Utc>,
    pub updated_at: DateTime<Utc>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, sqlx::FromRow)]
pub struct JobApplication {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub job_id: Uuid,
    pub applicant_name: String,
    pub applicant_email: String,
    pub state: String, // applied, reviewed, accepted, rejected, withdrawn
    pub cv_media_id: Option<Uuid>,
    pub created_at: DateTime<Utc>,
    pub updated_at: DateTime<Utc>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, sqlx::FromRow)]
pub struct JobChatThread {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub job_id: Uuid,
    pub application_id: Uuid,
    pub created_at: DateTime<Utc>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, sqlx::FromRow)]
pub struct JobChatMessage {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub thread_id: Uuid,
    pub sender_id: Uuid,
    pub content: String,
    pub is_from_merchant: bool,
    pub created_at: DateTime<Utc>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
pub struct CreateJobPostRequest {
    pub title: String,
    pub description: String,
    pub category: String,
    pub contact_phone: String,
    pub is_confidential: Option<bool>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
pub struct SubmitApplicationRequest {
    pub applicant_name: String,
    pub applicant_email: String,
    pub cv_media_id: Option<Uuid>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
pub struct SendChatMessageRequest {
    pub content: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct JobPostResponse {
    pub id: String,
    pub title: String,
    pub state: String,
    pub created_at: String,
}

===== backend/src/jobs/mod.rs
use axum::{
    extract::{Path, State},
    http::StatusCode,
    routing::{get, post},
    Json, Router,
};
use uuid::Uuid;

use crate::auth::SessionClaim;
use crate::error::AppError;
use crate::state::AppState;

use super::manager::SupportManager;
use super::models::*;

pub fn support_routes() -> Router<AppState> {
    Router::new()
        .route("/stores/:store_id/tickets", post(create_ticket).get(list_tickets))
        .route("/tickets/:ticket_id", get(get_ticket))
        .route("/tickets/:ticket_id/reply", post(reply_ticket))
}

async fn create_ticket(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim { consumer_id, .. }: SessionClaim,
    Path(store_id): Path<Uuid>,
    Json(req): Json<CreateTicketRequest>,
) -> Result<(StatusCode, Json<TicketResponse>), AppError> {
    let ticket = SupportManager::create_ticket(&state.db, consumer_id, Some(store_id), req).await?;

    Ok((
        StatusCode::CREATED,
        Json(TicketResponse {
            id: ticket.id.to_string(),
            subject: ticket.subject,
            state: ticket.state,
            created_at: ticket.created_at.to_rfc3339(),
        }),
    ))
}

async fn get_ticket(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    Path(ticket_id): Path<Uuid>,
) -> Result<Json<Ticket>, AppError> {
    let ticket = SupportManager::get_ticket(&state.db, ticket_id).await?;
    Ok(Json(ticket))
}

async fn list_tickets(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim { consumer_id, .. }: SessionClaim,
    Path(store_id): Path<Uuid>,
) -> Result<Json<Vec<Ticket>>, AppError> {
    let tickets = SupportManager::list_consumer_tickets(&state.db, store_id, consumer_id).await?;
    Ok(Json(tickets))
}

async fn reply_ticket(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim { account_id, .. }: SessionClaim,
    Path(ticket_id): Path<Uuid>,
    Json(req): Json<ReplyToTicketRequest>,
) -> Result<(StatusCode, Json<TicketReply>), AppError> {
    let reply = SupportManager::reply_to_ticket(&state.db, ticket_id, account_id, true, req).await?;
    Ok((StatusCode::CREATED, Json(reply)))
}

===== backend/src/jobs/routes.rs
use axum::{
    extract::{Path, State},
    http::StatusCode,
    routing::{get, post},
    Json, Router,
};
use uuid::Uuid;

use crate::auth::SessionClaim;
use crate::error::AppError;
use crate::state::AppState;

use super::manager::JobsManager;
use super::models::*;

pub fn jobs_routes() -> Router<AppState> {
    Router::new()
        .route("/jobs", get(list_public_jobs))
        .route("/stores/:store_id/jobs", post(create_job).get(list_store_jobs))
        .route("/jobs/:job_id", get(get_job))
        .route("/jobs/:job_id/apply", post(apply_to_job))
        .route("/jobs/applications/:app_id/chat", post(send_message))
}

async fn list_public_jobs(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
) -> Result<Json<Vec<JobPost>>, AppError> {
    let jobs = JobsManager::list_public_jobs(&state.db).await?;
    Ok(Json(jobs))
}

async fn create_job(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim { account_id, .. }: SessionClaim,
    Path(store_id): Path<Uuid>,
    Json(req): Json<CreateJobPostRequest>,
) -> Result<(StatusCode, Json<JobPostResponse>), AppError> {
    // TODO: Verify store ownership
    let job = JobsManager::create_job_post(&state.db, store_id, req).await?;

    Ok((
        StatusCode::CREATED,
        Json(JobPostResponse {
            id: job.id.to_string(),
            title: job.title,
            state: job.state,
            created_at: job.created_at.to_rfc3339(),
        }),
    ))
}

async fn get_job(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    Path(job_id): Path<Uuid>,
) -> Result<Json<JobPost>, AppError> {
    let job = JobsManager::get_job_post(&state.db, job_id).await?;
    Ok(Json(job))
}

async fn list_store_jobs(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim { account_id, .. }: SessionClaim,
    Path(store_id): Path<Uuid>,
) -> Result<Json<Vec<JobPost>>, AppError> {
    // TODO: Verify store ownership
    let jobs: Vec<JobPost> = sqlx::query_as(
        "SELECT * FROM app.job_post WHERE store_id = $1 ORDER BY created_at DESC",
    )
    .bind(&store_id)
    .fetch_all(&state.db)
    .await
    .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

    Ok(Json(jobs))
}

async fn apply_to_job(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    Path(job_id): Path<Uuid>,
    Json(req): Json<SubmitApplicationRequest>,
) -> Result<(StatusCode, Json<JobApplication>), AppError> {
    let app = JobsManager::apply_to_job(&state.db, job_id, req).await?;
    Ok((StatusCode::CREATED, Json(app)))
}

async fn send_message(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim { account_id, .. }: SessionClaim,
    Path(app_id): Path<Uuid>,
    Json(req): Json<SendChatMessageRequest>,
) -> Result<(StatusCode, Json<JobChatMessage>), AppError> {
    // Get thread ID from application
    let thread: (Uuid,) = sqlx::query_as(
        "SELECT thread_id FROM app.job_chat_thread WHERE application_id = $1",
    )
    .bind(&app_id)
    .fetch_one(&state.db)
    .await
    .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

    let msg = JobsManager::send_message(&state.db, thread.0, account_id, true, req).await?;
    Ok((StatusCode::CREATED, Json(msg)))
}

===== backend/src/media/manager.rs
use crate::error::{AppError, ErrorCode};
use crate::audit::AuditLogger;
use uuid::Uuid;
use sqlx::PgPool;
use chrono::Utc;
use std::sync::Arc;

use super::models::*;

pub struct MediaManager;

impl MediaManager {
    const DEFAULT_QUOTA_BYTES: i64 = 3_000_000_000; // 3 GB
    const MAX_FILE_SIZE: i64 = 100_000_000; // 100 MB for video
    const NEW_ACCOUNT_DAILY_LIMIT: i64 = 500_000_000; // 500 MB

    /// Create a presigned upload URL for a media object.
    pub async fn create_upload_url(
        pool: &PgPool,
        account_id: Uuid,
        store_id: Uuid,
        req: GetUploadUrlRequest,
    ) -> Result<UploadUrlResponse, AppError> {
        // Verify store ownership
        let _: (Uuid,) = sqlx::query_as(
            "SELECT id FROM app.store WHERE id = $1 AND account_id = $2 AND deleted_at IS NULL",
        )
        .bind(&store_id)
        .bind(&account_id)
        .fetch_optional(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?
        .ok_or_else(|| AppError::new(ErrorCode::NotFound, "Store not found"))?;

        // Check quota
        let quota: MediaQuota = sqlx::query_as(
            "SELECT store_id, quota_bytes, used_bytes, updated_at FROM app.media_quota WHERE store_id = $1"
        )
        .bind(&store_id)
        .fetch_optional(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?
        .unwrap_or(MediaQuota {
            store_id,
            quota_bytes: Self::DEFAULT_QUOTA_BYTES,
            used_bytes: 0,
            updated_at: Utc::now(),
        });

        if quota.used_bytes + req.file_size > quota.quota_bytes {
            return Err(AppError::new(
                ErrorCode::QuotaExceeded,
                "Upload would exceed quota",
            ));
        }

        if req.file_size > Self::MAX_FILE_SIZE {
            return Err(AppError::new(
                ErrorCode::InvalidInput,
                "File size exceeds maximum",
            ));
        }

        // Create media record
        let media_id = Uuid::new_v4();
        let s3_key = format!("staging/{}/{}/{}", store_id, Uuid::new_v4(), req.file_name);
        let now = Utc::now();

        sqlx::query(
            r#"
            INSERT INTO app.media (id, store_id, file_name, file_size, mime_type, s3_key_staging, guardduty_scan_status, is_promoted, created_at, updated_at)
            VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, 'pending', false, $7, $7)
            "#,
        )
        .bind(&media_id)
        .bind(&store_id)
        .bind(&req.file_name)
        .bind(req.file_size)
        .bind(&req.mime_type)
        .bind(&s3_key)
        .bind(&now)
        .execute(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        // TODO: Generate presigned URL from S3Manager
        // For now, return stub
        let upload_url = format!("https://s3.amazonaws.com/rabita-staging/{}?signed", s3_key);

        Ok(UploadUrlResponse {
            upload_url,
            media_id: media_id.to_string(),
            s3_key,
        })
    }

    /// Handle GuardDuty scan status change.
    /// Called by EventBridge on scan_status tag change.
    pub async fn on_scan_status_changed(
        pool: &PgPool,
        media_id: Uuid,
        s3_key: String,
        scan_status: &str,
    ) -> Result<(), AppError> {
        match scan_status {
            "NO_THREATS_FOUND" => {
                // Promote to media bucket
                Self::promote_to_media(pool, media_id, &s3_key).await?;
            }
            "THREATS_FOUND" => {
                // Move to quarantine
                Self::quarantine_media(pool, media_id, &s3_key).await?;
            }
            "UNSUPPORTED" | "ACCESS_DENIED" | "FAILED" => {
                // Hold and alert
                Self::hold_media(pool, media_id, scan_status).await?;
            }
            _ => {
                // Unknown status: default-deny
                Self::hold_media(pool, media_id, "UNKNOWN").await?;
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    async fn promote_to_media(
        pool: &PgPool,
        media_id: Uuid,
        s3_key_staging: &str,
    ) -> Result<(), AppError> {
        let media_key = s3_key_staging.replace("staging/", "media/");
        let now = Utc::now();

        sqlx::query(
            r#"
            UPDATE app.media
            SET s3_key_media = $1, guardduty_scan_status = 'no_threats_found', is_promoted = true, updated_at = $2
            WHERE id = $3
            "#,
        )
        .bind(&media_key)
        .bind(&now)
        .bind(&media_id)
        .execute(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        // TODO: S3 copy operation and quota update
        Ok(())
    }

    async fn quarantine_media(
        pool: &PgPool,
        media_id: Uuid,
        _s3_key_staging: &str,
    ) -> Result<(), AppError> {
        let now = Utc::now();

        sqlx::query(
            "UPDATE app.media SET guardduty_scan_status = 'threats_found', updated_at = $1 WHERE id = $2",
        )
        .bind(&now)
        .bind(&media_id)
        .execute(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        // TODO: Alert admin, audit log
        Ok(())
    }

    async fn hold_media(
        pool: &PgPool,
        media_id: Uuid,
        status: &str,
    ) -> Result<(), AppError> {
        let now = Utc::now();

        sqlx::query(
            "UPDATE app.media SET guardduty_scan_status = $1, updated_at = $2 WHERE id = $3",
        )
        .bind(status)
        .bind(&now)
        .bind(&media_id)
        .execute(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        // TODO: Alert and audit
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Get media.
    pub async fn get_media(
        pool: &PgPool,
        account_id: Uuid,
        store_id: Uuid,
        media_id: Uuid,
    ) -> Result<Media, AppError> {
        // Verify store ownership
        let _: (Uuid,) = sqlx::query_as(
            "SELECT id FROM app.store WHERE id = $1 AND account_id = $2 AND deleted_at IS NULL",
        )
        .bind(&store_id)
        .bind(&account_id)
        .fetch_optional(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?
        .ok_or_else(|| AppError::new(ErrorCode::NotFound, "Store not found"))?;

        sqlx::query_as::<_, Media>("SELECT * FROM app.media WHERE id = $1 AND store_id = $2")
            .bind(&media_id)
            .bind(&store_id)
            .fetch_optional(pool)
            .await
            .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?
            .ok_or_else(|| AppError::new(ErrorCode::NotFound, "Media not found"))
    }

    /// Get quota.
    pub async fn get_quota(
        pool: &PgPool,
        account_id: Uuid,
        store_id: Uuid,
    ) -> Result<MediaQuota, AppError> {
        // Verify store ownership
        let _: (Uuid,) = sqlx::query_as(
            "SELECT id FROM app.store WHERE id = $1 AND account_id = $2 AND deleted_at IS NULL",
        )
        .bind(&store_id)
        .bind(&account_id)
        .fetch_optional(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?
        .ok_or_else(|| AppError::new(ErrorCode::NotFound, "Store not found"))?;

        let quota: Option<MediaQuota> = sqlx::query_as(
            "SELECT store_id, quota_bytes, used_bytes, updated_at FROM app.media_quota WHERE store_id = $1",
        )
        .bind(&store_id)
        .fetch_optional(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        Ok(quota.unwrap_or(MediaQuota {
            store_id,
            quota_bytes: Self::DEFAULT_QUOTA_BYTES,
            used_bytes: 0,
            updated_at: Utc::now(),
        }))
    }
}

===== backend/src/media/routes.rs
use axum::{
    extract::{Path, State},
    http::StatusCode,
    routing::{get, post},
    Json, Router,
};
use uuid::Uuid;

use crate::auth::SessionClaim;
use crate::error::AppError;
use crate::state::AppState;

use super::manager::MediaManager;
use super::models::*;

pub fn media_routes() -> Router<AppState> {
    Router::new()
        .route("/stores/:store_id/media/upload-url", post(get_upload_url))
        .route("/stores/:store_id/media/:media_id", get(get_media))
        .route("/stores/:store_id/media/quota", get(get_quota))
}

async fn get_upload_url(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim { account_id, .. }: SessionClaim,
    Path(store_id): Path<Uuid>,
    Json(req): Json<GetUploadUrlRequest>,
) -> Result<(StatusCode, Json<UploadUrlResponse>), AppError> {
    let url = MediaManager::create_upload_url(&state.db, account_id, store_id, req).await?;
    Ok((StatusCode::CREATED, Json(url)))
}

async fn get_media(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim { account_id, .. }: SessionClaim,
    Path((store_id, media_id)): Path<(Uuid, Uuid)>,
) -> Result<Json<Media>, AppError> {
    let media = MediaManager::get_media(&state.db, account_id, store_id, media_id).await?;
    Ok(Json(media))
}

async fn get_quota(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim { account_id, .. }: SessionClaim,
    Path(store_id): Path<Uuid>,
) -> Result<Json<QuotaResponse>, AppError> {
    let quota = MediaManager::get_quota(&state.db, account_id, store_id).await?;
    let percent_used = (quota.used_bytes as f64 / quota.quota_bytes as f64) * 100.0;

    Ok(Json(QuotaResponse {
        quota_bytes: quota.quota_bytes,
        used_bytes: quota.used_bytes,
        percent_used,
    }))
}

===== backend/src/media/service.rs
// backend/src/media/service.rs

//! Media service per D-037, D-104: quota enforcement, GuardDuty scanning.

use crate::error::{AppError, AppResult};
use sqlx::PgPool;
use uuid::Uuid;

pub struct MediaManager;

impl MediaManager {
    /// Request presigned upload URL per D-037.
    /// Validates against quota and per-file limits before issuing URL.
    pub async fn request_upload_url(
        db: &PgPool,
        store_id: Uuid,
        media_type: &str,
        declared_size_bytes: i64,
    ) -> AppResult<(String, Uuid)> {
        // Validate media type
        if !matches!(media_type, "image" | "video" | "pdf" | "other") {
            return Err(AppError::BadRequest("Invalid media type".to_string()));
        }

        // Check file size limits per D-037
        let max_size = match media_type {
            "image" => 20 * 1024 * 1024, // 20 MB
            "video" => 100 * 1024 * 1024, // 100 MB
            "pdf" => 20 * 1024 * 1024, // 20 MB
            _ => 50 * 1024 * 1024, // 50 MB
        };

        if declared_size_bytes > max_size {
            return Err(AppError::BadRequest(format!(
                "File size exceeds limit for {}",
                media_type
            )));
        }

        // Check quota per D-037
        let quota: (i64, i64) = sqlx::query_as(
            r#"
            SELECT quota_bytes, used_bytes FROM app.media_quota WHERE store_id = $1
            "#,
        )
        .bind(store_id)
        .fetch_one(db)
        .await?;

        let (quota_bytes, used_bytes) = quota;

        if used_bytes + declared_size_bytes > quota_bytes {
            return Err(AppError::BadRequest("Quota exceeded".to_string()));
        }

        // Create media record in 'pending' state
        let media_id: Uuid = sqlx::query_scalar(
            r#"
            INSERT INTO app.media (store_id, media_type, file_size_bytes, guarduty_scan_status)
            VALUES ($1, $2, $3, 'pending')
            RETURNING id
            "#,
        )
        .bind(store

===== backend/src/middleware/auth_extractor.rs
// backend/src/middleware/auth_extractor.rs

//! Axum extractors for authenticated sessions.

use crate::{
    auth::session::{SessionClaim, SessionManager},
    error::{AppError, AppResult},
    state::AppState,
};
use axum::{
    async_trait,
    extract::{FromRequestParts, TypedHeader},
    headers::{authorization::Bearer, Authorization},
    http::request::Parts,
};

/// Extractor for session claim from Bearer token.
#[async_trait]
impl FromRequestParts<AppState> for SessionClaim {
    type Rejection = AppError;

    async fn from_request_parts(
        parts: &mut Parts,
        state: &AppState,
    ) -> Result<Self, Self::Rejection> {
        let TypedHeader(Authorization::<Bearer>(bearer)) =
            TypedHeader::<Authorization<Bearer>>::from_request_parts(parts, state)
                .await
                .map_err(|_| AppError::Unauthorized)?;

        SessionManager::verify_session(&state.cache, bearer.token())
            .await
    }
}

===== backend/src/notifications/manager.rs
use crate::error::AppError;
use uuid::Uuid;
use sqlx::PgPool;
use chrono::Utc;

use super::models::*;

pub struct NotificationManager;

impl NotificationManager {
    /// Log notification send per D-102
    pub async fn log_notification(
        db: &PgPool,
        consumer_id: Option<Uuid>,
        order_id: Option<Uuid>,
        notification_type: &str,
        email_address: &str,
        ses_message_id: &str,
    ) -> Result<(), AppError> {
        let id = Uuid::new_v4();
        let now = Utc::now();

        sqlx::query(
            r#"
            INSERT INTO app.notification_log (id, consumer_id, order_id, notification_type, email_address, delivery_status, ses_message_id, created_at, updated_at)
            VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, 'sent', $6, $7, $7)
            "#,
        )
        .bind(&id)
        .bind(&consumer_id)
        .bind(&order_id)
        .bind(notification_type)
        .bind(email_address)
        .bind(ses_message_id)
        .bind(&now)
        .execute(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        Ok(())
    }

    /// Build order confirmation email per D-102
    pub fn build_order_confirmation_email(
        store_name: &str,
        public_order_number: &str,
        total_egp: i64,
        recipient_email: &str,
    ) -> EmailNotification {
        let total_egp_str = format!("{},{:.2}", total_egp / 100, total_egp % 100);

        EmailNotification {
            notification_type: "order_placed".to_string(),
            recipient_email: recipient_email.to_string(),
            subject: format!("Order {} confirmed", public_order_number),
            html_body: format!(
                r#"<html><body><h1>Order Confirmation</h1>
                <p>Thank you for your order at {}!</p>
                <p><strong>Order Number:</strong> {}</p>
                <p><strong>Total:</strong> {} ج.م</p>
                </body></html>"#,
                store_name, public_order_number, total_egp_str
            ),
        }
    }

    /// Build refund approval email per D-102
    pub fn build_refund_approval_email(
        public_order_number: &str,
        refund_amount_egp: i64,
        reason: &str,
        recipient_email: &str,
    ) -> EmailNotification {
        let amount_str = format!("{},{:.2}", refund_amount_egp / 100, refund_amount_egp % 100);

        EmailNotification {
            notification_type: "refund_approved".to_string(),
            recipient_email: recipient_email.to_string(),
            subject: format!("Refund approved for order {}", public_order_number),
            html_body: format!(
                r#"<html><body><h1>Refund Approved</h1>
                <p>Your refund for order {} has been approved.</p>
                <p><strong>Amount:</strong> {} ج.م</p>
                <p><strong>Reason:</strong> {}</p>
                </body></html>"#,
                public_order_number, amount_str, reason
            ),
        }
    }
}

===== backend/src/onboarding/application.rs
// backend/src/onboarding/application.rs

//! Merchant application lifecycle per D-039 with CAPTCHA/proof-of-work and rate limits.

use crate::error::{AppError, AppResult};
use sqlx::PgPool;
use uuid::Uuid;

pub struct ApplicationManager;

impl ApplicationManager {
    /// Submit merchant application per D-039.
    /// Email verification required before entering queue.
    /// CAPTCHA/proof-of-work required per D-039.
    /// Rate limits: 3 per IP per 24h, 1 per email per 24h.
    pub async fn submit_application(
        db: &PgPool,
        business_name: &str,
        contact_name: &str,
        contact_phone: &str,
        email: &str,
        product_category: Option<&str>,
        notes: Option<&str>,
        ip_address: Option<&str>,
        user_agent: Option<&str>,
        captcha_verified: bool,
    ) -> AppResult<Uuid> {
        // Check CAPTCHA/proof-of-work
        if !captcha_verified {
            return Err(AppError::BadRequest(
                "CAPTCHA verification required".to_string(),
            ));
        }

        // Check IP rate limit: 3 per 24h
        let ip_count: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar(
            r#"
            SELECT COUNT(*) FROM app.merchant_application
            WHERE ip_address = $1::inet
            AND created_at > now() - interval '24 hours'
            "#,
        )
        .bind(ip_address)
        .fetch_one(db)
        .await
        .unwrap_or(0);

        if ip_count >= 3 {
            return Err(AppError::BadRequest(
                "Rate limit exceeded: 3 applications per IP per 24 hours".to_string(),
            ));
        }

        // Check email rate limit: 1 per 24h
        let email_count: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar(
            r#"
            SELECT COUNT(*) FROM app.merchant_application
            WHERE email = $1
            AND created_at > now() - interval '24 hours'
            "#,
        )
        .bind(email)
        .fetch_one(db)
        .await
        .unwrap_or(0);

        if email_count >= 1 {
            return Err(AppError::BadRequest(
                "Rate limit exceeded: 1 application per email per 24 hours".to_string(),
            ));
        }

        // Create application in 'submitted' state
        let app_id: Uuid = sqlx::query_scalar(
            r#"
            INSERT INTO app.merchant_application
            (business_name, contact_name, contact_phone, email, product_category, notes,
             ip_address, user_agent, state, expires_at)
            VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, $7, $8, 'submitted', now() + interval '90 days')
            RETURNING id
            "#,
        )
        .bind(business_name)
        .bind(contact_name)
        .bind(contact_phone)
        .bind(email)
        .bind(product_category)
        .bind(notes)
        .bind(ip_address)
        .bind(user_agent)
        .fetch_one(db)
        .await?;

        Ok(app_id)
    }

    /// Send email verification link per D-039.
    pub async fn send_verification_email(email: &str, app_id: Uuid) -> AppResult<()> {
        // TODO: Generate signed token
        // TODO: Send SES email with verification link
        tracing::info!("Email verification sent to {}", email);
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Verify email and move to queue per D-039.
    pub async fn verify_email(db: &PgPool, app_id: Uuid, token: &str) -> AppResult<()> {
        // TODO: Verify signed token
        // TODO: Update application.email_verified_at

        sqlx::query(
            r#"
            UPDATE app.merchant_application
            SET email_verified_at = now()
            WHERE id = $1
            "#,
        )
        .bind(app_id)
        .execute(db)
        .await?;

        Ok(())
    }

    /// Approve application per D-039 (super-admin only).
    pub async fn approve(db: &PgPool, app_id: Uuid, reviewer_id: Uuid) -> AppResult<()> {
        sqlx::query(
            r#"
            UPDATE app.merchant_application
            SET state = 'approved', reviewer_id = $1, reviewed_at = now()
            WHERE id = $2 AND state IN ('submitted', 'contacted')
            "#,
        )
        .bind(reviewer_id)
        .bind(app_id)
        .execute(db)
        .await?;

        Ok(())
    }

    /// Reject application per D-039 (super-admin only).
    /// Rejection is durable for repeat-abuse detection.
    pub async fn reject(
        db: &PgPool,
        app_id: Uuid,
        reviewer_id: Uuid,
        reason: &str,
    ) -> AppResult<()> {
        sqlx::query(
            r#"
            UPDATE app.merchant_application
            SET state = 'rejected', reviewer_id = $1, reviewed_at = now(), rejection_reason = $2
            WHERE id = $3
            "#,
        )
        .bind(reviewer_id)
        .bind(reason)
        .bind(app_id)
        .execute(db)
        .await?;

        Ok(())
    }

    /// Get application by ID.
    pub async fn get(db: &PgPool, app_id: Uuid) -> AppResult<ApplicationRow> {
        sqlx::query_as::<_, ApplicationRow>(
            r#"
            SELECT id, business_name, contact_name, contact_phone, email, product_category,
                   notes, ip_address, user_agent, state, rejection_reason, reviewer_id,
                   created_at, reviewed_at, expires_at
            FROM app.merchant_application
            WHERE id = $1
            "#,
        )
        .bind(app_id)
        .fetch_optional(db)
        .await?
        .ok_or_else(|| AppError::NotFound("Application not found".to_string()))
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, sqlx::FromRow)]
pub struct ApplicationRow {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub business_name: String,
    pub contact_name: String,
    pub contact_phone: String,
    pub email: String,
    pub product_category: Option<String>,
    pub notes: Option<String>,
    pub ip_address: Option<String>,
    pub user_agent: Option<String>,
    pub state: String,
    pub rejection_reason: Option<String>,
    pub reviewer_id: Option<uuid::Uuid>,
    pub created_at: chrono::DateTime<chrono::Utc>,
    pub reviewed_at: Option<chrono::DateTime<chrono::Utc>>,
    pub expires_at: Option<chrono::DateTime<chrono::Utc>>,
}

===== backend/src/onboarding/provisioning.rs
// backend/src/onboarding/provisioning.rs

//! Manual credential provisioning per D-045: phone-delivered initial code, 24h TTL.

use crate::error::{AppError, AppResult};
use crate::auth::password::PasswordManager;
use sqlx::PgPool;
use uuid::Uuid;
use rand::Rng;

pub struct ProvisioningManager;

impl ProvisioningManager {
    /// Generate merchant identifier and temporary password per D-045.
    /// Identifier format: "mer-" + 12 random base32 chars (excluding ambiguous L/I/O).
    /// Password: 12-16 chars from base32 alphabet, no ambiguous chars.
    pub fn generate_credentials() -> (String, String) {
        const ALPHABET: &[u8] = b"023456789ABCDEFGHJKMNPQRSTVWXYZ"; // No L, I, O, U
        let mut rng = rand::thread_rng();

        // Generate identifier: "mer-" + 12 random chars
        let mut identifier = String::from("mer-");
        for _ in 0..12 {
            let idx = rng.gen_range(0..ALPHABET.len());
            identifier.push(ALPHABET[idx] as char);
        }

        // Generate temp password: 12-16 chars
        let password_len = rng.gen_range(12..=16);
        let mut password = String::new();
        for _ in 0..password_len {
            let idx = rng.gen_range(0..ALPHABET.len());
            password.push(ALPHABET[idx] as char);
        }

        (identifier, password)
    }

    /// Generate initial 8-digit code to be delivered by phone per D-045.
    /// 15-minute TTL, single-use, burned on first attempt.
    pub fn generate_initial_code() -> String {
        let mut rng = rand::thread_rng();
        let code = rng.gen_range(10000000..=99999999);
        code.to_string()
    }

    /// Create account from approved application per D-045.
    /// Requires manual phone call; no automated notification per D-045.
    /// Credentials delivered during phone call only.
    pub async fn provision_merchant(
        db: &PgPool,
        app_id: Uuid,
        provisioner_id: Uuid,
    ) -> AppResult<(String, String, String)> {
        // Verify application is approved
        let app: super::application::ApplicationRow =
            super::application::ApplicationManager::get(db, app_id).await?;

        if app.state != "approved" {
            return Err(AppError::BadRequest(
                "Application must be approved before provisioning".to_string(),
            ));
        }

        // Generate identifier, password, initial code
        let (identifier, temp_password) = Self::generate_credentials();
        let initial_code = Self::generate_initial_code();

        // Hash password
        let password_hash = PasswordManager::hash_password(&temp_password)?;

        // Hash initial code for storage
        let code_hash = PasswordManager::hash_password(&initial_code)?;

        // Create account within transaction
        let mut tx = db.begin().await?;

        let account_id: Uuid = sqlx::query_scalar(
            r#"
            INSERT INTO app.account (identifier, email, password_hash)
            VALUES ($1, $2, $3)
            RETURNING id
            "#,
        )
        .bind(&identifier)
        .bind(&app.email)
        .bind(&password_hash)
        .fetch_one(&mut *tx)
        .await?;

        // Create merchant role binding
        sqlx::query(
            r#"
            INSERT INTO app.account_role (account_id, role_name, context_id)
            VALUES ($1, 'merchant', gen_random_uuid())
            "#,
        )
        .bind(account_id)
        .execute(&mut *tx)
        .await?;

        // Store initial code (in production, KMS-encrypted)
        sqlx::query(
            r#"
            INSERT INTO app.recovery_code (account_id, code_hash, used_at)
            VALUES ($1, $2, NULL)
            "#,
        )
        .bind(account_id)
        .bind(&code_hash)
        .execute(&mut *tx)
        .await?;

        // Update application state to 'invited'
        sqlx::query(
            r#"
            UPDATE app.merchant_application
            SET state = 'invited'
            WHERE id = $1
            "#,
        )
        .bind(app_id)
        .execute(&mut *tx)
        .await?;

        // Audit provisioning
        sqlx::query(
            r#"
            INSERT INTO app.audit_log
            (event_type, actor_id, actor_type, target_id, target_type, reason, created_at)
            VALUES ('account_provisioned', $1, 'account', $2, 'account', 'Merchant provisioned', now())
            "#,
        )
        .bind(provisioner_id)
        .bind(account_id)
        .execute(&mut *tx)
        .await?;

        tx.commit().await?;

        // Log (not credentials!) that provisioning succeeded
        tracing::info!(
            "Merchant account provisioned: {} for application {}",
            identifier,
            app_id
        );

        Ok((identifier, temp_password, initial_code))
    }

    /// Store initial code in session for verification per D-045.
    /// Code is 8 digits, 15-minute TTL, single-use.
    pub async fn issue_initial_code_challenge(
        cache: &redis::aio::ConnectionManager,
        account_id: Uuid,
    ) -> AppResult<()> {
        let key = format!("initial_code:{}", account_id);
        let mut conn = cache.clone();

        // Code already stored in recovery_code table; this is just marking it active in Valkey
        redis::cmd("SET")
            .arg(&key)
            .arg("pending")
            .arg("EX")
            .arg(900) // 15 minutes
            .query_async::<_, ()>(&mut conn)
            .await?;

        Ok(())
    }
}

===== backend/src/onboarding/routes.rs
// backend/src/onboarding/routes.rs

//! Onboarding routes per D-039 (application submission, verification).

use crate::{
    error::AppResult,
    onboarding::{application::ApplicationManager, models::{MerchantApplicationInput, MerchantApplicationResponse}},
    state::AppState,
};
use axum::{
    extract::{Path, State},
    http::StatusCode,
    Json,
};
use uuid::Uuid;

pub fn router() -> axum::Router<AppState> {
    axum::Router::new()
        .route("/applications/submit", axum::routing::post(submit_application))
        .route("/applications/:id/verify-email", axum::routing::post(verify_email))
}

/// POST /api/v1/onboarding/applications/submit
/// Submit merchant application per D-039.
async fn submit_application(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    axum::extract::ConnectInfo(addr): axum::extract::ConnectInfo<std::net::SocketAddr>,
    Json(input): Json<MerchantApplicationInput>,
) -> AppResult<(StatusCode, Json<MerchantApplicationResponse>)> {
    let ip = addr.ip().to_string();

    // TODO: Verify CAPTCHA/proof-of-work

    let app_id = ApplicationManager::submit_application(
        &state.db,
        &input.business_name,
        &input.contact_name,
        &input.contact_phone,
        &input.email,
        input.product_category.as_deref(),
        input.notes.as_deref(),
        Some(&ip),
        None,
        true, // captcha_verified (TODO: real verification)
    )
    .await?;

    // Send verification email
    ApplicationManager::send_verification_email(&input.email, app_id).await?;

    Ok((
        StatusCode::CREATED,
        Json(MerchantApplicationResponse {
            id: app_id,
            state: "submitted".to_string(),
            created_at: chrono::Utc::now(),
        }),
    ))
}

/// POST /api/v1/onboarding/applications/:id/verify-email
/// Verify email and enter approval queue per D-039.
async fn verify_email(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    Path(app_id): Path<Uuid>,
    axum::Json(body): Json<serde_json::json!({ "token": String })>,
) -> AppResult<StatusCode> {
    ApplicationManager::verify_email(&state.db, app_id, &body["token"].as_str().unwrap_or(""))
        .await?;

    Ok(StatusCode::OK)
}

//! Onboarding tests.

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::super::*;

    #[test]
    fn test_generate_credentials() {
        let (identifier, password) = provisioning::ProvisioningManager::generate_credentials();

        assert!(identifier.starts_with("mer-"));
        assert_eq!(identifier.len(), 16); // "mer-" + 12 chars
        assert!(password.len() >= 12 && password.len() <= 16);

        // Verify no ambiguous chars
        assert!(!identifier.contains('L'));
        assert!(!identifier.contains('I'));
        assert!(!identifier.contains('O'));
        assert!(!identifier.contains('U'));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_generate_initial_code() {
        let code = provisioning::ProvisioningManager::generate_initial_code();

        assert_eq!(code.len(), 8);
        assert!(code.parse::<u64>().is_ok());
        let num = code.parse::<u64>().unwrap();
        assert!(num >= 10000000 && num <= 99999999);
    }

===== backend/src/orders/manager.rs
use crate::error::{AppError, ErrorCode};
use crate::idempotency::IdempotencyStore;
use crate::checkout::CheckoutManager;
use crate::cart::{Cart, CartManager};
use uuid::Uuid;
use sqlx::PgPool;
use chrono::Utc;
use serde_json::json;

use super::models::*;
use super::stock_reserve::StockReserveManager;

pub struct OrderManager;

impl OrderManager {
    /// Create order with idempotency per D-130 (principal-scoped key + payload hash)
    /// Returns the public order number
    pub async fn create_order(
        db: &PgPool,
        store_id: Uuid,
        consumer_email: &str,
        principal_type: &str,        // "consumer" or "guest_session"
        principal_id: &str,           // consumer UUID or session ID
        idempotency_key: &str,
        req: CreateOrderRequest,
        cart: Cart,
        checkout_summary: CheckoutSummary,
    ) -> Result<Order, AppError> {
        // Check idempotency (per D-130: (principal_type, principal_id, key) scope)
        let payload_hash = format!(
            "{}{}{}{}",
            store_id, consumer_email, req.delivery_address, checkout_summary.total_egp
        );
        let payload_hash = sha2::Sha256::digest(payload_hash.as_bytes())
            .to_vec();
        let payload_hash_hex = hex::encode(&payload_hash);

        let idempotency_result: Option<(Option<String>, Option<String>)> = sqlx::query_as(
            "SELECT response_status, response_body FROM app.idempotency_key WHERE principal_type = $1 AND principal_id = $2 AND key = $3",
        )
        .bind(principal_type)
        .bind(principal_id)
        .bind(idempotency_key)
        .fetch_optional(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        if let Some((status, body)) = idempotency_result {
            if let Some(body_str) = body {
                // Return cached response
                let cached: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(&body_str)
                    .map_err(|e| AppError::new(
                        ErrorCode::InternalError,
                        &format!("JSON parse: {}", e),
                    ))?;
                // TODO: Parse and return the cached order
                return Ok(Order {
                    id: Uuid::new_v4(),
                    store_id,
                    public_order_number: cached["public_order_number"]
                        .as_str()
                        .unwrap_or("")
                        .to_string(),
                    consumer_email: consumer_email.to_string(),
                    consumer_phone: None,
                    verification_method: "email_only".to_string(),
                    order_snapshot: json!({}),
                    order_status: "pending".to_string(),
                    payment_status: "delayed".to_string(),
                    delivery_governorate: req.delivery_governorate.clone(),
                    delivery_county: req.delivery_county.clone(),
                    delivery_district: req.delivery_district.clone(),
                    delivery_address: req.delivery_address.clone(),
                    delivery_coords_lat: req.delivery_coords_lat,
                    delivery_coords_lng: req.delivery_coords_lng,
                    branch_pickup_id: req.branch_pickup_id,
                    rounding_delta_egp: checkout_summary.rounding_delta_egp,
                    created_at: Utc::now(),
                    updated_at: Utc::now(),
                });
            } else if status.as_deref() == Some("409") {
                return Err(AppError::new(
                    ErrorCode::Conflict,
                    "Payload mismatch for idempotency key",
                ));
            }
        }

        // Create order in transaction
        let mut tx = db.begin().await.map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        let order_id = Uuid::new_v4();
        let public_order_number = Self::generate_public_order_number(&mut tx, store_id)
            .await?;

        let now = Utc::now();

        // Build order snapshot per D-114
        let order_snapshot = OrderSnapshot {
            lines: checkout_summary.line_items.iter().map(|item| {
                OrderLineSnapshot {
                    product_id: item.product_id.clone(),
                    product_name: item.product_name.clone(),
                    product_image_s3_key: None, // TODO: fetch from product
                    variant_id: item.variant_id.clone(),
                    variant_name: item.variant_name.clone(),
                    sku: None, // TODO: fetch
                    unit_price_egp: item.unit_price_egp,
                    quantity: item.quantity,
                    line_total_egp: item.line_total_egp,
                    line_tax_egp: item.line_tax_egp,
                    branch_id: "".to_string(), // TODO: from line
                }
            }).collect(),
            subtotal_egp: checkout_summary.subtotal_egp,
            tax_egp: checkout_summary.tax_egp,
            shipping_cost_egp: checkout_summary.shipping_cost_egp,
            total_egp: checkout_summary.total_egp,
            tax_rate_bp: 0, // TODO: fetch from settings
        };

        let snapshot_json = serde_json::to_value(&order_snapshot)
            .map_err(|e| AppError::new(ErrorCode::InternalError, &e.to_string()))?;

        sqlx::query(
            r#"
            INSERT INTO app.order (id, store_id, public_order_number, consumer_email, consumer_phone, verification_method, order_snapshot, order_status, payment_status, delivery_governorate, delivery_county, delivery_district, delivery_address, delivery_coords_lat, delivery_coords_lng, branch_pickup_id, rounding_delta_egp, created_at, updated_at)
            VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, 'email_only', $6, 'pending', 'delayed', $7, $8, $9, $10, $11, $12, $13, $14, $15, $15)
            "#,
        )
        .bind(&order_id)
        .bind(&store_id)
        .bind(&public_order_number)
        .bind(consumer_email)
        .bind(&req.phone)
        .bind(&snapshot_json)
        .bind(&req.delivery_governorate)
        .bind(&req.delivery_county)
        .bind(&req.delivery_district)
        .bind(&req.delivery_address)
        .bind(req.delivery_coords_lat)
        .bind(req.delivery_coords_lng)
        .bind(&req.branch_pickup_id)
        .bind(checkout_summary.rounding_delta_egp)
        .bind(&now)
        .execute(&mut *tx)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        // Create pending stock reserves per D-051
        let reserve_lines: Vec<_> = cart.lines.iter().map(|l| {
            (l.product_id, l.variant_id, l.branch_id, l.quantity)
        }).collect();

        StockReserveManager::create_pending_reserves(&mut *tx, order_id, reserve_lines)
            .await?;

        // Record idempotency key
        let response_json = json!({
            "id": order_id.to_string(),
            "public_order_number": public_order_number,
            "status": "pending",
        });

        sqlx::query(
            r#"
            INSERT INTO app.idempotency_key (principal_type, principal_id, key, payload_hash, response_status, response_body, created_at)
            VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, '201', $5, $6)
            ON CONFLICT (principal_type, principal_id, key) DO NOTHING
            "#,
        )
        .bind(principal_type)
        .bind(principal_id)
        .bind(idempotency_key)
        .bind(&payload_hash_hex)
        .bind(response_json.to_string())
        .bind(&now)
        .execute(&mut *tx)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        tx.commit().await.map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        Ok(Order {
            id: order_id,
            store_id,
            public_order_number,
            consumer_email: consumer_email.to_string(),
            consumer_phone: req.phone,
            verification_method: "email_only".to_string(),
            order_snapshot: snapshot_json,
            order_status: "pending".to_string(),
            payment_status: "delayed".to_string(),
            delivery_governorate: req.delivery_governorate,
            delivery_county: req.delivery_county,
            delivery_district: req.delivery_district,
            delivery_address: req.delivery_address,
            delivery_coords_lat: req.delivery_coords_lat,
            delivery_coords_lng: req.delivery_coords_lng,
            branch_pickup_id: req.branch_pickup_id,
            rounding_delta_egp: checkout_summary.rounding_delta_egp,
            created_at: now,
            updated_at: now,
        })
    }

    /// Generate public order number per D-120: <slug>-<base32-6>
    async fn generate_public_order_number(
        tx: &mut sqlx::Transaction<'_, sqlx::Postgres>,
        store_id: Uuid,
    ) -> Result<String, AppError> {
        let slug: (String,) = sqlx::query_as(
            "SELECT slug FROM app.store WHERE id = $1",
        )
        .bind(&store_id)
        .fetch_one(&mut **tx)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        let base32_suffix = Self::random_base32_6chars();
        Ok(format!("{}-{}", slug.0, base32_suffix))
    }

    fn random_base32_6chars() -> String {
        use rand::Rng;
        const CHARSET: &[u8] = b"ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ234567";
        let mut rng = rand::thread_rng();

        (0..6)
            .map(|_| {
                let idx = rng.gen_range(0..CHARSET.len());
                CHARSET[idx] as char
            })
            .collect()
    }

    /// Get order by ID
    pub async fn get_order(
        db: &PgPool,
        store_id: Uuid,
        order_id: Uuid,
    ) -> Result<Order, AppError> {
        sqlx::query_as::<_, Order>(
            "SELECT * FROM app.order WHERE id = $1 AND store_id = $2",
        )
        .bind(&order_id)
        .bind(&store_id)
        .fetch_optional(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?
        .ok_or_else(|| AppError::new(ErrorCode::NotFound, "Order not found"))
    }

    /// List orders for store
    pub async fn list_orders(
        db: &PgPool,
        store_id: Uuid,
    ) -> Result<Vec<Order>, AppError> {
        sqlx::query_as::<_, Order>(
            "SELECT * FROM app.order WHERE store_id = $1 ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 100",
        )
        .bind(&store_id)
        .fetch_all(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))
    }

    /// Confirm order: transition reserves to active, decrement stock per D-051
    pub async fn confirm_order(
        db: &PgPool,
        store_id: Uuid,
        order_id: Uuid,
    ) -> Result<(), AppError> {
        // Activate stock reserves and decrement
        StockReserveManager::activate_reserves_and_decrement_stock(db, order_id).await?;

        // Update order status to confirmed-category (e.g., "confirmed")
        sqlx::query(
            "UPDATE app.order SET order_status = 'confirmed', updated_at = now() WHERE id = $1 AND store_id = $2",
        )
        .bind(&order_id)
        .bind(&store_id)
        .execute(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        Ok(())
    }

    /// Cancel order: release stock per D-051
    pub async fn cancel_order(
        db: &PgPool,
        store_id: Uuid,
        order_id: Uuid,
    ) -> Result<(), AppError> {
        StockReserveManager::release_stock(db, order_id).await?;

        sqlx::query(
            "UPDATE app.order SET order_status = 'cancelled', updated_at = now() WHERE id = $1 AND store_id = $2",
        )
        .bind(&order_id)
        .bind(&store_id)
        .execute(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        Ok(())
    }
}

===== backend/src/orders/models.rs
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use uuid::Uuid;
use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};
use sqlx::types::JsonValue;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, sqlx::FromRow)]
pub struct Order {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub store_id: Uuid,
    pub public_order_number: String, // <slug>-<base32-6> per D-120
    pub consumer_email: String,
    pub consumer_phone: Option<String>,
    pub verification_method: String, // email_only, whatsapp_otp, phone_not_on_whatsapp
    pub order_snapshot: JsonValue, // Immutable per D-114
    pub order_status: String,       // Platform category
    pub payment_status: String,
    pub delivery_governorate: String,
    pub delivery_county: String,
    pub delivery_district: String,
    pub delivery_address: String,
    pub delivery_coords_lat: Option<f64>,
    pub delivery_coords_lng: Option<f64>,
    pub branch_pickup_id: Option<Uuid>,
    pub rounding_delta_egp: i64,
    pub created_at: DateTime<Utc>,
    pub updated_at: DateTime<Utc>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct OrderSnapshot {
    pub lines: Vec<OrderLineSnapshot>,
    pub subtotal_egp: i64,
    pub tax_egp: i64,
    pub shipping_cost_egp: i64,
    pub total_egp: i64,
    pub tax_rate_bp: i32,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct OrderLineSnapshot {
    pub product_id: String,
    pub product_name: String,
    pub product_image_s3_key: Option<String>,
    pub variant_id: Option<String>,
    pub variant_name: Option<String>,
    pub sku: Option<String>,
    pub unit_price_egp: i64,
    pub quantity: i64,
    pub line_total_egp: i64,
    pub line_tax_egp: i64,
    pub branch_id: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, sqlx::FromRow)]
pub struct OrderItem {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub order_id: Uuid,
    pub product_id: Uuid,
    pub variant_id: Option<Uuid>,
    pub quantity: i64,
    pub unit_price_egp: i64,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct StockReserve {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub order_id: Uuid,
    pub product_id: Uuid,
    pub variant_id: Option<Uuid>,
    pub branch_id: Uuid,
    pub quantity: i64,
    pub state: String, // pending, active, released, shadowed
    pub created_at: DateTime<Utc>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
pub struct CreateOrderRequest {
    pub email: String,
    pub phone: Option<String>,
    pub delivery_governorate: String,
    pub delivery_county: String,
    pub delivery_district: String,
    pub delivery_address: String,
    pub delivery_coords_lat: Option<f64>,
    pub delivery_coords_lng: Option<f64>,
    pub branch_pickup_id: Option<Uuid>,
    pub coupon_code: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct OrderResponse {
    pub id: String,
    pub public_order_number: String,
    pub status: String,
    pub payment_status: String,
    pub total_egp: i64,
    pub created_at: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
pub struct UpdateOrderStatusRequest {
    pub status: String,
    pub note: Option<String>,
}

===== backend/src/orders/routes.rs
use axum::{
    extract::{Path, State},
    http::StatusCode,
    routing::{get, post},
    Json, Router,
};
use uuid::Uuid;

use crate::{
    auth::SessionClaim,
    cart::CartManager,
    checkout::CheckoutManager,
    error::AppError,
    state::AppState,
};

use super::manager::OrderManager;
use super::models::*;

pub fn order_routes() -> Router<AppState> {
    Router::new()
        .route("/stores/:store_id/orders", post(create_order).get(list_orders))
        .route("/stores/:store_id/orders/:order_id", get(get_order))
        .route("/stores/:store_id/orders/:order_id/confirm", post(confirm_order))
        .route("/stores/:store_id/orders/:order_id/cancel", post(cancel_order))
}

async fn create_order(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim {
        consumer_id,
        account_id,
        ..
    }: SessionClaim,
    Path(store_id): Path<Uuid>,
    Json(req): Json<CreateOrderRequest>,
) -> Result<(StatusCode, Json<OrderResponse>), AppError> {
    let consumer_key = consumer_id
        .unwrap_or_else(|| account_id.to_string());

    let mut cache = state.cache.get_connection().await.map_err(|e| {
        AppError::new(
            crate::error::ErrorCode::CacheError,
            &format!("Connection error: {}", e),
        )
    })?;

    // Get cart
    let cart = CartManager::get_cart(&mut cache, store_id, &consumer_key).await?;

    // Calculate checkout
    let summary = CheckoutManager::calculate_checkout(
        &state.db,
        store_id,
        &cart,
        &req.delivery_governorate,
        &req.delivery_county,
        &req.delivery_district,
        req.branch_pickup_id,
        req.coupon_code.as_deref(),
    )
    .await?;

    // Create order with idempotency
    let order = OrderManager::create_order(
        &state.db,
        store_id,
        &req.email,
        "consumer",
        &consumer_key,
        "default-key", // TODO: from header
        req,
        cart,
        summary,
    )
    .await?;

    // Clear cart
    CartManager::clear_cart(&mut cache, store_id, &consumer_key)
        .await
        .ok();

    Ok((
        StatusCode::CREATED,
        Json(OrderResponse {
            id: order.id.to_string(),
            public_order_number: order.public_order_number,
            status: order.order_status,
            payment_status: order.payment_status,
            total_egp: summary.total_egp,
            created_at: order.created_at.to_rfc3339(),
        }),
    ))
}

async fn get_order(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim { account_id, .. }: SessionClaim,
    Path((store_id, order_id)): Path<(Uuid, Uuid)>,
) -> Result<Json<Order>, AppError> {
    // TODO: Verify store ownership or consumer access
    let order = OrderManager::get_order(&state.db, store_id, order_id).await?;
    Ok(Json(order))
}

async fn list_orders(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim { account_id, .. }: SessionClaim,
    Path(store_id): Path<Uuid>,
) -> Result<Json<Vec<Order>>, AppError> {
    // TODO: Verify store ownership
    let orders = OrderManager::list_orders(&state.db, store_id).await?;
    Ok(Json(orders))
}

async fn confirm_order(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim { account_id, .. }: SessionClaim,
    Path((store_id, order_id)): Path<(Uuid, Uuid)>,
) -> Result<StatusCode, AppError> {
    // TODO: Verify store ownership
    OrderManager::confirm_order(&state.db, store_id, order_id).await?;
    Ok(StatusCode::OK)
}

async fn cancel_order(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    SessionClaim { account_id, .. }: SessionClaim,
    Path((store_id, order_id)): Path<(Uuid, Uuid)>,
) -> Result<StatusCode, AppError> {
    // TODO: Verify store ownership + cancellation permission
    OrderManager::cancel_order(&state.db, store_id, order_id).await?;
    Ok(StatusCode::OK)
}

===== backend/src/orders/stock_reserve.rs
use crate::error::{AppError, ErrorCode};
use uuid::Uuid;
use sqlx::PgPool;
use chrono::Utc;

use super::models::StockReserve;

pub struct StockReserveManager;

impl StockReserveManager {
    /// Create pending stock reserves at order creation per D-051
    pub async fn create_pending_reserves(
        db: &PgPool,
        order_id: Uuid,
        lines: Vec<(Uuid, Option<Uuid>, Uuid, i64)>, // (product_id, variant_id, branch_id, qty)
    ) -> Result<Vec<StockReserve>, AppError> {
        let now = Utc::now();
        let mut reserves = vec![];

        for (product_id, variant_id, branch_id, quantity) in lines {
            let reserve_id = Uuid::new_v4();

            sqlx::query(
                r#"
                INSERT INTO app.stock_reserve (id, order_id, product_id, variant_id, branch_id, quantity, state, created_at)
                VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, 'pending', $7)
                "#,
            )
            .bind(&reserve_id)
            .bind(&order_id)
            .bind(&product_id)
            .bind(&variant_id)
            .bind(&branch_id)
            .bind(quantity)
            .bind(&now)
            .execute(db)
            .await
            .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

            reserves.push(StockReserve {
                id: reserve_id,
                order_id,
                product_id,
                variant_id,
                branch_id,
                quantity,
                state: "pending".to_string(),
                created_at: now,
            });
        }

        Ok(reserves)
    }

    /// Transition reserves to active and decrement stock atomically per D-051
    pub async fn activate_reserves_and_decrement_stock(
        db: &PgPool,
        order_id: Uuid,
    ) -> Result<(), AppError> {
        let mut tx = db.begin().await.map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        // Get all pending reserves for this order
        let reserves: Vec<(Uuid, Uuid, Option<Uuid>, Uuid, i64)> = sqlx::query_as(
            "SELECT id, product_id, variant_id, branch_id, quantity FROM app.stock_reserve WHERE order_id = $1 AND state = 'pending'",
        )
        .bind(&order_id)
        .fetch_all(&mut *tx)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        let now = Utc::now();

        for (reserve_id, product_id, variant_id, branch_id, quantity) in reserves {
            // Lock and check availability
            let current_stock: (i64,) = sqlx::query_as(
                "SELECT stock_qty FROM app.branch_product WHERE branch_id = $1 AND product_id = $2 FOR UPDATE",
            )
            .bind(&branch_id)
            .bind(&product_id)
            .fetch_one(&mut *tx)
            .await
            .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

            if current_stock.0 < quantity {
                return Err(AppError::new(
                    ErrorCode::InvalidState,
                    &format!("Insufficient stock for product {}", product_id),
                ));
            }

            // Decrement stock
            sqlx::query(
                "UPDATE app.branch_product SET stock_qty = stock_qty - $1 WHERE branch_id = $2 AND product_id = $3",
            )
            .bind(quantity)
            .bind(&branch_id)
            .bind(&product_id)
            .execute(&mut *tx)
            .await
            .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

            // Transition reserve to active
            sqlx::query(
                "UPDATE app.stock_reserve SET state = 'active' WHERE id = $1",
            )
            .bind(&reserve_id)
            .execute(&mut *tx)
            .await
            .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;
        }

        tx.commit().await.map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        Ok(())
    }

    /// Release stock (cancellation, return, failure) per D-051
    pub async fn release_stock(
        db: &PgPool,
        order_id: Uuid,
    ) -> Result<(), AppError> {
        let mut tx = db.begin().await.map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        // Get all active reserves
        let reserves: Vec<(Uuid, Uuid, i64)> = sqlx::query_as(
            "SELECT id, branch_id, product_id, quantity FROM app.stock_reserve WHERE order_id = $1 AND state = 'active'",
        )
        .bind(&order_id)
        .fetch_all(&mut *tx)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        for (reserve_id, branch_id, product_id, quantity) in reserves {
            // Restore stock
            sqlx::query(
                "UPDATE app.branch_product SET stock_qty = stock_qty + $1 WHERE branch_id = $2 AND product_id = $3",
            )
            .bind(quantity)
            .bind(&branch_id)
            .bind(&product_id)
            .execute(&mut *tx)
            .await
            .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

            // Transition to released
            sqlx::query(
                "UPDATE app.stock_reserve SET state = 'released' WHERE id = $1",
            )
            .bind(&reserve_id)
            .execute(&mut *tx)
            .await
            .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;
        }

        tx.commit().await.map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        Ok(())
    }
}

===== backend/src/outbox/manager.rs
use crate::error::AppError;
use uuid::Uuid;
use sqlx::PgPool;
use chrono::Utc;

use super::models::*;

pub struct OutboxManager;

impl OutboxManager {
    const MAX_RETRIES: i32 = 5;
    const LEASE_DURATION_SECONDS: i64 = 60;
    const INITIAL_BACKOFF_MS: i64 = 100;

    /// Enqueue event per D-084
    pub async fn enqueue_event(
        db: &PgPool,
        event_type: &str,
        aggregate_id: Uuid,
        payload: serde_json::Value,
    ) -> Result<Uuid, AppError> {
        let id = Uuid::new_v4();
        let now = Utc::now();

        sqlx::query(
            r#"
            INSERT INTO app.outbox (id, event_type, aggregate_id, payload, state, attempt_count, created_at, updated_at)
            VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, 'pending', 0, $5, $5)
            "#,
        )
        .bind(&id)
        .bind(event_type)
        .bind(&aggregate_id)
        .bind(&payload)
        .bind(&now)
        .execute(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        Ok(id)
    }

    /// Claim pending events with lease per D-084
    pub async fn claim_events(
        db: &PgPool,
        worker_id: &str,
        batch_size: i32,
    ) -> Result<Vec<OutboxEvent>, AppError> {
        let now = Utc::now();
        let lease_until = now + chrono::Duration::seconds(Self::LEASE_DURATION_SECONDS);

        // Claim expired and new leases
        let events: Vec<OutboxEvent> = sqlx::query_as(
            r#"
            SELECT * FROM app.outbox
            WHERE state = 'pending'
            AND (lease_until IS NULL OR lease_until < now())
            ORDER BY created_at ASC
            LIMIT $1
            FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
            "#,
        )
        .bind(batch_size)
        .fetch_all(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        // Update lease
        for event in &events {
            sqlx::query(
                "UPDATE app.outbox SET state = 'processing', lease_until = $1, updated_at = $2 WHERE id = $3",
            )
            .bind(&lease_until)
            .bind(&now)
            .bind(&event.id)
            .execute(db)
            .await
            .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;
        }

        Ok(events)
    }

    /// Mark event as completed
    pub async fn mark_completed(db: &PgPool, event_id: Uuid) -> Result<(), AppError> {
        let now = Utc::now();

        sqlx::query(
            "UPDATE app.outbox SET state = 'completed', lease_until = NULL, updated_at = $1 WHERE id = $2",
        )
        .bind(&now)
        .bind(&event_id)
        .execute(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        Ok(())
    }

    /// Mark event as failed with retry or DLQ
    pub async fn mark_failed(
        db: &PgPool,
        event_id: Uuid,
        error_message: &str,
    ) -> Result<(), AppError> {
        let mut tx = db.begin().await.map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        let event: (i32,) = sqlx::query_as(
            "SELECT attempt_count FROM app.outbox WHERE id = $1",
        )
        .bind(&event_id)
        .fetch_one(&mut *tx)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        let now = Utc::now();

        // Log attempt
        sqlx::query(
            "INSERT INTO app.outbox_attempt (id, event_id, attempt_number, status, error_message, created_at) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, 'failure', $4, $5)",
        )
        .bind(Uuid::new_v4())
        .bind(&event_id)
        .bind(event.0 + 1)
        .bind(error_message)
        .bind(&now)
        .execute(&mut *tx)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        if event.0 >= Self::MAX_RETRIES {
            // Move to DLQ
            sqlx::query(
                "UPDATE app.outbox SET state = 'dlq', lease_until = NULL, attempt_count = $1, last_error = $2, updated_at = $3 WHERE id = $4",
            )
            .bind(event.0 + 1)
            .bind(error_message)
            .bind(&now)
            .bind(&event_id)
            .execute(&mut *tx)
            .await
            .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;
        } else {
            // Retry with backoff
            let backoff_ms = Self::INITIAL_BACKOFF_MS * 2i64.pow(event.0 as u32);
            let lease_until = now + chrono::Duration::milliseconds(backoff_ms);

            sqlx::query(
                "UPDATE app.outbox SET state = 'pending', lease_until = $1, attempt_count = $2, last_error = $3, updated_at = $4 WHERE id = $5",
            )
            .bind(&lease_until)
            .bind(event.0 + 1)
            .bind(error_message)
            .bind(&now)
            .bind(&event_id)
            .execute(&mut *tx)
            .await
            .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;
        }

        tx.commit().await.map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        Ok(())
    }
}

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use uuid::Uuid;
use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, sqlx::FromRow)]
pub struct OutboxEvent {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub event_type: String, // order_created, order_confirmed, refund_approved, ticket_created, etc.
    pub aggregate_id: Uuid,  // order_id, etc.
    pub payload: serde_json::Value,
    pub state: String, // pending, processing, completed, failed, dlq
    pub lease_until: Option<DateTime<Utc>>, // For crash recovery per D-084
    pub attempt_count: i32,
    pub last_error: Option<String>,
    pub created_at: DateTime<Utc>,
    pub updated_at: DateTime<Utc>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, sqlx::FromRow)]
pub struct OutboxAttempt {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub event_id: Uuid,
    pub attempt_number: i32,
    pub status: String, // success, failure
    pub error_message: Option<String>,
    pub created_at: DateTime<Utc>,
}

===== backend/src/promotions/manager.rs
use crate::error::{AppError, ErrorCode};
use uuid::Uuid;
use sqlx::PgPool;
use chrono::Utc;

use super::models::*;

pub struct PromotionManager;

impl PromotionManager {
    /// Create coupon per D-053
    pub async fn create_coupon(
        db: &PgPool,
        store_id: Uuid,
        req: CreateCouponRequest,
    ) -> Result<Coupon, AppError> {
        // Validate that either amount or percent is specified
        if req.discount_amount_egp.is_none() && req.discount_percent_bp.is_none() {
            return Err(AppError::new(
                ErrorCode::InvalidInput,
                "Either discount amount or percent must be specified",
            ));
        }

        let id = Uuid::new_v4();
        let now = Utc::now();

        sqlx::query(
            r#"
            INSERT INTO app.coupon (id, store_id, code, discount_amount_egp, discount_percent_bp, branch_id, is_active, max_uses, current_uses, expires_at, created_at)
            VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, true, $7, 0, $8, $9)
            "#,
        )
        .bind(&id)
        .bind(&store_id)
        .bind(&req.code)
        .bind(req.discount_amount_egp)
        .bind(req.discount_percent_bp)
        .bind(&req.branch_id)
        .bind(req.max_uses)
        .bind(req.expires_at.as_deref())
        .bind(&now)
        .execute(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        Self::get_coupon(db, store_id, &req.code).await
    }

    /// Get coupon by code
    pub async fn get_coupon(
        db: &PgPool,
        store_id: Uuid,
        code: &str,
    ) -> Result<Coupon, AppError> {
        sqlx::query_as::<_, Coupon>(
            "SELECT * FROM app.coupon WHERE store_id = $1 AND code = $2 AND is_active = true",
        )
        .bind(&store_id)
        .bind(code)
        .fetch_optional(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?
        .ok_or_else(|| AppError::new(ErrorCode::NotFound, "Coupon not found or inactive"))
    }

    /// Apply coupon (increment usage)
    pub async fn apply_coupon(
        db: &PgPool,
        store_id: Uuid,
        code: &str,
    ) -> Result<i64, AppError> {
        let coupon = Self::get_coupon(db, store_id, code).await?;

        // Check max uses
        if let Some(max) = coupon.max_uses {
            if coupon.current_uses >= max {
                return Err(AppError::new(
                    ErrorCode::InvalidState,
                    "Coupon usage limit exceeded",
                ));
            }
        }

        // Check expiry
        if let Some(expires) = coupon.expires_at {
            if expires < Utc::now() {
                return Err(AppError::new(
                    ErrorCode::InvalidState,
                    "Coupon has expired",
                ));
            }
        }

        // Increment usage
        sqlx::query(
            "UPDATE app.coupon SET current_uses = current_uses + 1 WHERE id = $1",
        )
        .bind(&coupon.id)
        .execute(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        // Return discount in piastres
        Ok(coupon.discount_amount_egp.unwrap_or(0))
    }

    /// Create bundle per D-053
    pub async fn create_bundle(
        db: &PgPool,
        store_id: Uuid,
        req: CreateBundleRequest,
    ) -> Result<Bundle, AppError> {
        if req.product_ids.is_empty() {
            return Err(AppError::new(
                ErrorCode::InvalidInput,
                "Bundle must contain at least one product",
            ));
        }

        let id = Uuid::new_v4();
        let now = Utc::now();

        sqlx::query(
            r#"
            INSERT INTO app.bundle (id, store_id, name, description, discount_amount_egp, discount_percent_bp, allow_partial_removal, max_partial_applications, is_active, created_at)
            VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, $7, $8, true, $9)
            "#,
        )
        .bind(&id)
        .bind(&store_id)
        .bind(&req.name)
        .bind(&req.description)
        .bind(req.discount_amount_egp)
        .bind(req.discount_percent_bp)
        .bind(req.allow_partial_removal.unwrap_or(false))
        .bind(req.max_partial_applications.unwrap_or(1))
        .bind(&now)
        .execute(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;

        // Add bundle items
        for product_id in req.product_ids {
            sqlx::query(
                "INSERT INTO app.bundle_item (id, bundle_id, product_id, required_quantity) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, 1)",
            )
            .bind(Uuid::new_v4())
            .bind(&id)
            .bind(&product_id)
            .execute(db)
            .await
            .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?;
        }

        Self::get_bundle(db, store_id, id).await
    }

    /// Get bundle
    pub async fn get_bundle(
        db: &PgPool,
        store_id: Uuid,
        bundle_id: Uuid,
    ) -> Result<Bundle, AppError> {
        sqlx::query_as::<_, Bundle>(
            "SELECT * FROM app.bundle WHERE id = $1 AND store_id = $2 AND is_active = true",
        )
        .bind(&bundle_id)
        .bind(&store_id)
        .fetch_optional(db)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::database_error(&e))?
        .ok_or_else(|| AppError::new(ErrorCode::NotFound, "Bundle not found"))
    }
}

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use uuid::Uuid;
use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, sqlx::FromRow)]
pub struct Discount {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub store_id: Uuid,
    pub product_id: Uuid,
    pub discount_amount_egp: Option<i64>,     // EGP
    pub discount_percent_bp: Option<i32>,     // Basis points
    pub is_active: bool,
    pub created_at: DateTime<Utc>,
    pub updated_at: DateTime<Utc>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, sqlx::FromRow)]
pub struct Coupon {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub store_id: Uuid,
    pub code: String,
    pub discount_amount_egp: Option<i64>,
    pub discount_percent_bp: Option<i32>,
    pub branch_id: Option<Uuid>, // NULL for store-wide
    pub is_active: bool,
    pub max_uses: Option<i32>,
    pub current_uses: i32,
    pub expires_at: Option<DateTime<Utc>>,
    pub created_at: DateTime<Utc>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, sqlx::FromRow)]
pub struct Bundle {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub store_id: Uuid,
    pub name: String,
    pub description: Option<String>,
    pub discount_amount_egp: Option<i64>,
    pub discount_percent_bp: Option<i32>,
    pub allow_partial_removal: bool,
    pub max_partial_applications: i32,
    pub is_active: bool,
    pub created_at: DateTime<Utc>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, sqlx::FromRow)]
pub struct BundleItem {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub bundle_id: Uuid,
    pub product_id: Uuid,
    pub required_quantity: i64,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, sqlx::FromRow)]
pub struct BundlePartialApplication {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub bundle_id: Uuid,
    pub order_id: Uuid,
    pub removed_product_ids: Vec<String>, // JSON array of product IDs
    pub original_price_egp: i64,
    pub recomputed_price_egp: i64,
    pub discount_applied_egp: i64,
    pub created_at: DateTime<Utc>,
}

    pub discount_amount_egp: Option<i64>,
    pub discount_percent_bp: Option<i32>,
    pub branch_id: Option<Uuid>,
    pub max_uses: Option<i32>,
    pub expires_at: Option<String>,
}
